- Suggested change: Give the manager the negotiated protocol version (IMCPRequestContext.ProtocolVersion, populated by the processor from _meta for modern requests and from the session for legacy). In CreateToolJSON: if version < 2026-07-28 and outputSchema.type <> 'object' then omit outputSchema; if version = 2025-03-26 omit title/outputSchema/icons/_meta. In result building: if version < 2026-07-28 and structuredContent is not a TJSONObject then drop it (keep the text fallback); if version = 2025-03-26 drop structuredContent and downgrade resource_link blocks to text.

### GAP-TL-24 [minor, effort M] Schema generator lacks title/format/minimum/maximum/default/pattern/additionalProperties/$schema and emits properties:{} for no-parameter tools

- Requirements: TL-10, TL-08, TL-13
- Current: GenerateSchema emits only type/properties/required/description/enum/items (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas:31-105); attribute set is Optional/SchemaDescription/SchemaEnum (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:14-35); zero-property classes (TGetTimeParams, src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.GetTime.pas:12-13) yield {type:object, properties:{}} rather than the recommended {type:object, additionalProperties:false}. Property names are always LowerCase(Prop.Name) (line 135-138) with no override, while the deserializer accepts case/underscore-insensitive keys (Serializer.pas:73-91).
- Required: Any 2020-12 keyword MAY appear; no-parameter tools should preferably use additionalProperties:false; schemas SHOULD describe constraints so LLMs and validators can use them. (All MAY/recommended.)
- Compat impact: Additive attributes; emitting additionalProperties:false for empty classes is harmless. If additionalProperties:false is ever emitted for non-empty classes, keep the deserializer's lenient key matching or document the tightening. Consumers' existing attribute usage unchanged.
- Suggested change: Add attributes in MCPServer.Types: SchemaTitle, SchemaFormat('date-time'|'email'|...), SchemaMinimum/SchemaMaximum(Double), SchemaMinLength/MaxLength, SchemaPattern, SchemaDefault(string), SchemaName('jsonName') for explicit property names, class-level SchemaAdditionalProperties(False) and SchemaDialect('$schema URI'); generator emits them; TMCPSchemaValidator (GAP-TL-07) enforces them. Emit additionalProperties:false automatically when a params class has no properties.

### GAP-TL-25 [minor, effort S] Documentation gaps: supported schema dialect, result forms, error conventions

- Requirements: TL-10, TL-20, TL-21
- Current: README documents only string-returning TMCPToolBase<T> tools with [Optional]/[SchemaDescription] (README.md:222-283) and the four example tools (README.md:438-443); it does not mention TMCPToolBase<T,R>/outputSchema, the TJSONArray content passthrough, the 'Error:' isError convention, the JSON Schema dialect, or naming rules.
- Required: "Clients and servers **SHOULD** document which schema dialects they support"; library consumers need the contract for isError, content blocks, structured output and naming.
- Compat impact: Docs only.
- Suggested change: Add README sections: 'Tool naming rules', 'Input schema (JSON Schema 2020-12 subset generated from RTTI; supported attributes)', 'Returning results (text / TMCPToolResult / structured output via TMCPToolBase<T,R>)', 'Reporting errors (EMCPToolError vs EMCPProtocolError; legacy Error: prefix)', 'Annotations, icons, x-mcp-header', 'Protocol versions served (2025-03-26, 2025-06-18, 2025-11-25, 2026-07-28) and per-era differences'.

### GAP-TL-18 [optional, effort L] x-mcp-header not supported (no schema annotation, no Mcp-Param-* validation)

- Requirements: TL-15, TL-16, TL-08
- Current: TMCPSchemaGenerator recognises only SchemaDescription/SchemaEnum attributes (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas:73-85), so no tool can emit x-mcp-header; TMCPIdHTTPServer reads only Accept and Mcp-Session-Id headers (src/Server/MCPServer.IdHTTPServer.pas:333-337) and never inspects Mcp-Param-*, Mcp-Method or Mcp-Name; CORS Access-Control-Allow-Headers lists only Accept, Content-Type, Last-Event-ID, Mcp-Protocol-Version, Mcp-Session-Id (lines 259-260). Integer params currently map to 'number' (GAP-TL-08), which x-mcp-header forbids.
- Required: Servers MAY use x-mcp-header; if they do, the value constraints in TL-15 apply and the HTTP server MUST validate Mcp-Param-{Name} headers against the body (400 + -32020 HeaderMismatch), MUST reject invalid characters, MUST decode the base64 sentinel, and MUST NOT expect a header for absent/null values.
- Compat impact: Opt-in per property; no effect on tools that do not use the attribute. Requires GAP-TL-08 first, otherwise modern clients would drop any tool annotating an Integer property. Standard-header (Mcp-Method/Mcp-Name) validation itself belongs to the transport area.
- Suggested change: Add SchemaHeaderAttribute(Name) in MCPServer.Types; generator emits 'x-mcp-header': Name only for root-level string/integer/boolean properties, validating non-empty, tchar-only (RFC 9110: !#$%&'*+-.^_`|~ and alnum), no CR/LF, case-insensitive uniqueness, and raising EMCPSchemaError at schema build otherwise. Expose TMCPToolsManager.FindTool(const Name: string): IMCPTool and a cached list of header-annotated properties per tool. In TMCPIdHTTPServer.HandlePostRequest (modern requests only): parse method/params.name/arguments; for each annotated property with a non-null value in the body, require header Mcp-Param-{Name} (case-insensitive RawHeaders lookup), decode '=?base64?...?=' (TNetEncoding.Base64, UTF-8), compare (numeric compare for integers, 'true'/'false' for booleans); on mismatch/missing/invalid chars respond HTTP 400 with JSON-RPC error {code:-32020, message:'Header mismatch: ...'}; when the body value is absent or null, ignore the header. Add Mcp-Method, Mcp-Name, Mcp-Protocol-Version and the configured Mcp-Param-* names to Access-Control-Allow-Headers (no wildcard patterns allowed).

### GAP-TL-19 [optional, effort L] MRTR (InputRequiredResult) on tools/call not supported; tools cannot see _meta, client capabilities, inputResponses or requestState

- Requirements: TL-23, TL-04, TL-05
- Current: IMCPTool.Execute receives only the arguments object (src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Base.pas:18); TMCPToolsManager.ExtractToolNameAndArguments reads only 'name' and 'arguments' (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:96-118); 'inputResponses', 'requestState' and '_meta' (progressToken, io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities) are discarded. No result type other than CallToolResult can be produced.
- Required: Servers MAY return {resultType:'input_required', inputRequests?, requestState?}; if they do, the MUSTs of TL-23 apply (at least one field; capability check; integrity-protected requestState; no assumption of retry). Must never be emitted to legacy clients.
- Compat impact: Additive. IMCPTool unchanged; a new IMCPRequestContext parameter is provided through the new ExecuteWithParamsEx virtual (GAP-TL-15) and an optional IMCPToolContextAware interface for direct IMCPTool implementers. Legacy clients receive an isError text result instead of an input_required result.
- Suggested change: New unit MCPServer.Tool.Context.pas: IMCPRequestContext = interface with ProtocolVersion: string; IsModern: Boolean; ClientCapabilities: TJSONObject (from params._meta['io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities']; nil/empty for legacy); ProgressToken: TValue; Meta: TJSONObject; InputResponses: TJSONObject; RequestState: string; function HasClientCapability(const Path: string): Boolean (e.g. 'elicitation.form'). EMCPInputRequired = class(Exception) holding InputRequests: TJSONObject (owned) and RequestState: string, with builders TMCPInputRequests.AddFormElicitation(Key, Message, RequestedSchema) / AddUrlElicitation. TMCPToolsManager.CallTool builds the context from Params, catches EMCPInputRequired: modern → result {resultType:'input_required', inputRequests, requestState} (no ttlMs/cacheScope), after asserting each request's method is allowed by ClientCapabilities (drop or raise -32021 MissingRequiredClientCapability otherwise) and that at least one of the two fields is present; legacy → {content:[text 'This tool requires interactive input that the connected client does not support'], isError:true}. Provide TMCPRequestState.Seal(Payload: TJSONObject; const Key: TBytes; TtlSeconds; const RequestDigest: string): string and Open(...) using THashSHA2.GetHMACAsBytes (System.Hash) + base64url, embedding exp/method/tool-name/args-digest; Open raises EMCPProtocolError(-32602,'Invalid requestState') on failure. Settings: RequestStateKey (random per process if absent).

### GAP-TL-20 [optional, effort L] _meta.progressToken on tools/call is ignored; no way to emit notifications/progress

- Requirements: TL-24
- Current: progressToken is never read (ToolsManager.pas:96-118); the HTTP SSE POST path buffers a single event into ResponseInfo.ContentText (src/Server/MCPServer.IdHTTPServer.pas:470-510) and the stdio loop writes only the final response (src/Server/MCPServer.StdioTransport.pas:64-71), so notifications cannot precede a result.
- Required: Server MAY send notifications/progress; if it does, tokens MUST be active, progress MUST increase, notifications MUST stop after completion, rate limiting SHOULD apply. Choosing not to send is allowed (current behaviour is conformant but the library offers no opt-in).
- Compat impact: Additive; transport change (streaming writes) is owned by the transport area. Tools that never call ReportProgress are unaffected.
- Suggested change: IMCPRequestContext.ReportProgress(Progress: Double; Total: Double = -1; const Msg: string = '') enqueues {method:'notifications/progress', params:{progressToken, progress, total?, message?}} into a per-request TThreadedQueue<string> only when a progressToken was supplied; enforce monotonic progress and a min-interval throttle. Transports drain the queue: stdio writes each notification line before the response; HTTP SSE handler must switch to streaming writes on AContext.Connection.IOHandler (WriteLn of 'event: message\ndata: ...\n\n') before writing the final result event.

### Already conformant

- tools/list and tools/call are routed by capability name 'tools' and HandlesMethod (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:59-77).
- Tool definitions carry name, optional title (emitted only when it differs from name, matching BaseMetadata semantics), description, inputSchema and, for TMCPToolBase<T,R>, outputSchema (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:185-211; src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Base.pas:100-106, 227-230).
- Generated inputSchema has root type:'object' with 'properties' and 'required' and no '$schema', so it correctly defaults to JSON Schema 2020-12; every emitted keyword (type, properties, required, description, enum, items) is valid 2020-12 (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas:45-50, 66-69, 75-101).
- Enum constraints are emitted both from [SchemaEnum] and from Delphi enumeration RTTI, and invalid enum values are rejected with an actionable message (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas:79-91, 152-170; src/Protocol/MCPServer.Serializer.pas:261-291).
- Property order inside 'properties' follows declaration order via RTTI GetProperties, so a given tool's schema is stable across requests (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas:56).
- The tool set is built once in the constructor from TMCPRegistry and never mutated per request, satisfying 'MUST NOT vary per-connection or as a side effect of other requests' (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:46-51, 84-94).
- Input-validation failures (unknown parameter, invalid enum value) and exceptions thrown by tool logic are returned as tool execution errors with isError:true rather than protocol errors, in line with SEP-1303 and the CallToolResult.isError guidance (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Serializer.pas:111-118, 133-138, 285-288; src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:120-128, 148-167).
- isError is emitted as a JSON boolean and omitted when false, which the schema permits ('If not set, this is assumed to be false') (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:161-166, 175-180).
- Text content blocks have the correct {type:'text', text} shape (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:156-159).
- When a tool returns a TJSONArray it is passed through verbatim as 'content', so image/audio/resource blocks can already be emitted by hand-written tools (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:141-147).
- structuredContent is a clone of the tool's TJSONObject and the outputSchema is generated from the same class R, so field names (lower-cased) and primitive types line up for simple classes (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:171; src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Base.pas:193-195, 227-230; src/Protocol/MCPServer.Serializer.pas:159-173).
- Bundled tool names 'echo', 'get_time', 'list_files', 'calculate' satisfy the naming rules (1-128 chars, [A-Za-z0-9_.-]) (src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Echo.pas:37, GetTime.pas:32, ListFiles.pas:45, Calculate.pas:48).
- Tool name uniqueness is effectively enforced: TMCPRegistry keys by name and TMCPToolsManager.RegisterTool uses TDictionary.Add (src/Core/MCPServer.Registration.pas:55; src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:79-82).
- Schemas returned by IMCPTool.GetInputSchema/GetOutputSchema are cloned and freed by the manager, giving a consistent ownership contract for library consumers (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:196-209).
- No reserved/forbidden error codes are emitted: only -32700/-32601/-32603 are produced today (src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:27-32, 209-217), so nothing in the -32020..-32099 range or -32002/-32042 is misused.
- The server never dereferences $ref (no schema validation with $ref support exists), so the 'MUST NOT automatically dereference network $ref' rule is trivially met (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Schema.Generator.pas, src/Protocol/MCPServer.Serializer.pas).
- list_files implements a path-confinement access check on its input (src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.ListFiles.pas:62-69), an example of the 'Validate all tool inputs / access controls' MUST at tool level.

### Notes

- Envelope placement: resultType and _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo are cross-cutting (every result), so inject them once in TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest based on the request era; ttlMs/cacheScope are method-specific and belong in TMCPToolsManager (and the resources manager for its list/read methods). Emitting ttlMs/cacheScope unconditionally is safe for legacy clients because Result has additionalProperties:{} (open) in schema.json 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25; resultType could also be emitted unconditionally for the same reason, but keeping it modern-only is cleaner for dual-era tests.
- schema.json 2026-07-28 required sets that a validating modern client will enforce: ListToolsResult=[cacheScope, resultType, tools, ttlMs]; CallToolResult=[content, resultType]; Result=[resultType]. ServerCapabilities.tools has no additionalProperties:false in any revision, so the current supportsProgress/supportsCancellation keys are tolerated but meaningless.
- Era propagation without breaking IMCPCapabilityManager.ExecuteMethod(Method, Params): TValue: either (a) a threadvar/`TMCPRequestScope.Current: IMCPRequestContext` set by the processor around ExecuteMethodCall (works for Indy worker threads and stdio), or (b) an additive IMCPCapabilityManagerEx with ExecuteMethodEx(Method, Params, Ctx) that the processor prefers via Supports(). Custom managers written against the old interface keep compiling either way. For modern requests the version is in params._meta['io.modelcontextprotocol/protocolVersion']; for legacy it is the negotiated session version, which today lives only in TMCPCoreManager (src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:97-101 stores a session id but not the client's requested version).
- Thread-safety constraint for any new tool API: tool instances are singletons created once and shared by all Indy worker threads (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:84-94), so per-request state (context, progress, result builders) must be passed as parameters/return values, never stored in tool fields.
- JsonRpcProcessor error mapping is string-based ('not found' → -32601, otherwise -32603) (src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:209-217); a typed EMCPProtocolError(Code, Message, Data) is the prerequisite for -32602 (unknown tool/invalid params/invalid cursor), -32020 (HeaderMismatch), -32021 (MissingRequiredClientCapability) and -32022. JSON-RPC constants are duplicated in JsonRpcProcessor.pas:27-32 and IdHTTPServer.pas:94-99; centralise them in MCPServer.Types together with the new -32020..-32022 codes.
- Tool-logic exceptions vs server errors: ExecuteTool's catch-all (ToolsManager.pas:122-127) also converts genuine server faults (EAccessViolation, EOutOfMemory) into isError results; the spec lists 'Server errors' under protocol errors. Consider mapping EAccessViolation/EOutOfMemory/EInvalidPointer to -32603 while keeping application exceptions as isError. This is a judgement call and was not listed as a gap.
- Deserializer leniency: keys are matched case-insensitively and with underscores stripped (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Serializer.pas:73-91), so the wire contract is looser than the advertised schema ('includehidden' vs 'includeHidden' vs 'include_hidden'). Harmless today; if additionalProperties:false or a strict validator is introduced, either keep the leniency (validate after key normalisation) or document the tightening.
- Delphi TDictionary iteration order is deterministic for identical insertion sequences within a build, so the current tools/list order is stable within one process run; it is nevertheless neither registration order nor guaranteed across table growth or Delphi versions, hence GAP-TL-11 is rated minor.
- TValue.AsBoolean on a non-Boolean enumeration (Serializer.pas:346-356): whether it raises EInvalidCast or yields a bogus boolean depends on the RTL version; either way the output disagrees with the generated {type:string, enum} schema. Could not compile/execute Delphi in this Linux environment; GAP-TL-06 and GAP-TL-10(c) are derived from code reading and should be confirmed with a quick manual test.
- Non-object outputSchema/structuredContent (SEP-2106) is modern-only; the generic TMCPToolBase<T,R> always produces objects, so legacy interop is preserved by construction. The new flexibility is only reachable through hand-written TMCPToolBase.BuildSchema/Execute or TMCPToolResult.SetStructuredContent, which is why era-shaping (GAP-TL-23) is minor.
- 2025-11-25 legacy clients may send Tool.execution.taskSupport expectations and a `task` member in tools/call params (tasks were core-experimental in 2025-11-25 and became the io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks extension in 2026-07-28). Out of scope for this area; the tools manager currently ignores unknown params, which is acceptable because taskSupport defaults to 'forbidden'.
- MRTR capability check needs the client's per-request capabilities: elicitation.form / elicitation.url (still active), sampling and roots (deprecated as of 2026-07-28 per SEP-2577 but still allowed during the deprecation window). If a tool needs elicitation and the client did not declare it, return MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError (-32021, data.requiredCapabilities) or degrade to an isError text — the spec's MUST is only 'MUST NOT send inputRequests the client has not declared'.
- x-mcp-header and CORS: Access-Control-Allow-Headers does not accept prefix wildcards, so browser clients require the concrete Mcp-Param-* names (or '*' for non-credentialed requests); the list can be computed from the registered tools' header-annotated properties at Start. Int64 properties are JSON 'integer' but may exceed the ±2^53 safe range required for header mirroring; the generator should refuse [SchemaHeader] on Int64 or document the limit.
- ttlMs choice: the tool list is immutable for the process lifetime and listChanged is not declared, so any positive ttlMs is truthful; the spec's example uses 300000. cacheScope 'public' is correct because the list does not depend on the caller (no authorization/filtering exists). If per-user filtering is ever added via OnBeforeCallTool-style hooks, switch to 'private'.
- Suggested new units to keep the library API additive: MCPServer.Errors.pas (EMCPProtocolError, EMCPToolError, EMCPInputRequired, error-code constants), MCPServer.Tool.Result.pas (TMCPToolResult + content builders), MCPServer.Tool.Context.pas (IMCPRequestContext, TMCPRequestState HMAC helper), MCPServer.Schema.Validator.pas (TMCPSchemaValidator). Existing units keep their public signatures; IMCPTool is extended only through optional interfaces (IMCPToolMetadata, IMCPToolContextAware) discovered with Supports().
- Structured-text fallback: use compact TJSONObject.ToJSON (no whitespace) in the TextContent block, matching the spec example format; for TMCPToolBase<T,R> this also makes the result readable by 2025-03-26 clients that have no structuredContent.
- SEP-986 (the original naming SEP) allowed '/' and capped names at 64 characters; the published spec text (2025-11-25 and 2026-07-28 'Tool Names') is the authority: 128 characters and no '/'. Validate against the spec text.
- The TMCPTool/TMCPToolsResponse classes in src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:93-111 (InputSchema as string) are unused by the manager; they can stay for source compatibility but should not be extended — the JSON is built directly in TMCPToolsManager.CreateToolJSON.

### Requirements

- **TL-01** (MUST, both): "Servers that support tools **MUST** declare the `tools` capability" with shape {"tools": {"listChanged": true|false}}. `listChanged` is the only defined key. Modern era: declared in the server/discover result; legacy era: in the initialize result.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Capabilities' (identical sentence in docs/specification/2025-11-25/server/tools.mdx and 2025-06-18); schema.ts ServerCapabilities.tools = { listChanged?: boolean }_
- **TL-02** (MUST, modern): "Servers that declare the `tools` capability **MUST** respond to `tools/list` requests with the set of tools currently available to the requesting client. This set **MAY** be empty and **MAY** change over time ..., but **MUST NOT** vary per-connection or as a side effect of other requests on the connection. The set **MAY** vary by the authorization presented on the request".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Capabilities'_
- **TL-03** (SHOULD, modern): "Servers **SHOULD** return tools in a deterministic order (i.e., the same ordering across requests when the underlying set of tools has not changed). Deterministic ordering enables clients to reliably cache the tool list and improves LLM prompt cache hit rates".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Capabilities'; changelog.mdx 'Minor changes' item 3_
- **TL-04** (MUST, modern): "The `result` **MUST** include a `resultType` field to indicate the type of the result." "A `resultType` of `"complete"` indicates the request completed successfully"; "`"input_required"` indicates the request is incomplete and more information is needed". Applies to every tools/list and tools/call result. Legacy servers omit it and clients treat absence as "complete".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, headings 'Result Responses' and 'ResultType'; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts interface Result ('Servers implementing this protocol version MUST include this field'); schema.json 2026-07-28: CallToolResult.required=[content,resultType], ListToolsResult.required=[cacheScope,resultType,tools,ttlMs]_
- **TL-05** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST include caching hints on results with `resultType: "complete"` returned by the following operations: `server/discover`, `tools/list`, `prompts/list`, `resources/list`, `resources/templates/list`, `resources/read`"; "Servers **MUST** provide a `ttlMs` value that is `>= 0`"; `cacheScope` is required and is "public" ("appropriate for lists of tools ... when they are identical for all users") or "private". Interim `input_required` results "are not cacheable and carry no caching hints".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx, headings 'Cacheable Results' and 'Time-to-Live (TTL) Field'; schema.ts ListToolsResult extends PaginatedResult, CacheableResult { ttlMs: number (@minimum 0); cacheScope: "public"|"private" }_
- **TL-06** (MUST, modern): "Servers **MUST** apply the same `cacheScope` to all response pages for a given list request." Servers **MAY** return different `ttlMs` per page.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx, heading 'Interaction with Pagination'_
- **TL-07** (SHOULD, both): `tools/list` supports cursor pagination: request `params.cursor` (opaque string), response optional `nextCursor`. "Servers **SHOULD**: Provide stable cursors; Handle invalid cursors gracefully"; "Invalid cursors **SHOULD** result in an error with code -32602 (Invalid params)." Page size is server-determined; pagination itself is optional (omit nextCursor when complete).  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination.mdx, headings 'Operations Supporting Pagination', 'Implementation Guidelines', 'Error Handling' (same in 2025-11-25/2025-06-18)_
- **TL-08** (MUST, both): `inputSchema` "**MUST** be a valid JSON Schema object (not `null`)", "Defaults to 2020-12 if no `$schema` field is present", root `type` must be "object" ("Tool arguments are always JSON objects, so `type: "object"` is required at the root"). "For tools with no parameters, use one of these valid approaches: `{ "type": "object", "additionalProperties": false }` - **Recommended**; `{ "type": "object" }`".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Tool' (identical in 2025-11-25); schema.ts Tool.inputSchema: { $schema?: string; type: "object"; [key: string]: unknown }_
- **TL-09** (MAY, modern): Modern: "Beyond that, any JSON Schema 2020-12 keyword may appear alongside `type` — including composition keywords (`oneOf`, `anyOf`, `allOf`, `not`), conditional keywords (`if`/`then`/`else`), reference keywords (`$ref`, `$defs`, `$anchor`)"; outputSchema "can be any valid JSON Schema 2020-12" (array/primitive roots allowed). Legacy 2025-06-18/2025-11-25: inputSchema/outputSchema are limited to type/properties/required (+$schema in 2025-11-25) and outputSchema root MUST be `type: "object"`.  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts interface Tool (inputSchema/outputSchema doc comments); SEP /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/seps/2106-json-schema-2020-12.md 'Specification'; changelog.mdx minor change 10. Legacy restriction: schema/2025-11-25/schema.ts Tool.outputSchema 'Currently restricted to type: "object" at the root level' and inputSchema limited to $schema/type/properties/required_
- **TL-10** (MUST, both): "Clients and servers **MUST** support JSON Schema 2020-12 for schemas without an explicit `$schema` field"; "Clients and servers **MUST** validate schemas according to their declared or default dialect. They **MUST** handle unsupported dialects gracefully by returning an appropriate error"; "Schemas **MUST** be valid according to their declared or default dialect"; "Clients and servers **SHOULD** document which schema dialects they support"; "Implementors are RECOMMENDED to use JSON Schema 2020-12".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, headings 'Schema Dialect', 'Implementation Requirements', 'Schema Validation' (same text in docs/specification/2025-11-25/basic/index.mdx; absent from 2025-06-18 and 2025-03-26)_
- **TL-11** (MUST NOT, modern): "Implementations **MUST NOT** automatically dereference `$ref` values that resolve to a network URI." Opt-in fetching "**MUST** be disabled by default"; "Schemas that fail to validate due to an unresolved external `$ref` **SHOULD** be rejected rather than silently treated as permissive"; implementations "**SHOULD** apply reasonable bounds, such as a maximum schema depth, a cap on the total number of subschemas, or a per-validation time budget" when validating composition keywords.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, headings '$ref Resolution' and 'Composition-Keyword Resource Use'_
- **TL-12** (SHOULD, both): "Tool names **SHOULD** be between 1 and 128 characters in length (inclusive)." "Tool names **SHOULD** be considered case-sensitive." "The following **SHOULD** be the only allowed characters: uppercase and lowercase ASCII letters (A-Z, a-z), digits (0-9), underscore (_), hyphen (-), and dot (.)" "Tool names **SHOULD NOT** contain spaces, commas, or other special characters." "Tool names **SHOULD** be unique within a server." Server `name` "**SHOULD NOT** be relied upon for disambiguation".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Tool Names' (identical in 2025-11-25; originates from seps/986-specify-format-for-tool-names.md, whose 64-char/'/' variant was superseded by the spec text)_
- **TL-13** (MAY, both): Tool definition fields: `name` (required), `title` (optional; "Display name precedence order is: `title`, `annotations.title`, then `name`"), `description` (optional), `inputSchema` (required), `outputSchema` (optional), `icons` (optional), `annotations` (optional: `title`, `readOnlyHint` default false, `destructiveHint` default true, `idempotentHint` default false, `openWorldHint` default true), `_meta` (optional). "clients **MUST** consider tool annotations to be untrusted unless they come from trusted servers" (hints only).  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts interfaces Tool, ToolAnnotations, BaseMetadata; docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx heading 'Tool'. (title/outputSchema/_meta since 2025-06-18; icons since 2025-11-25; annotations since 2025-03-26)_
- **TL-14** (MAY, both): Tools MAY carry `icons`: array of `{ src (required URI: HTTPS URL or data: URI), mimeType?, sizes? (e.g. ["48x48"], ["any"]), theme? ("light"|"dark") }`. Clients "**MUST** reject icon URIs that use unsafe schemes" other than HTTPS/`data:` and MUST support image/png and image/jpeg, SHOULD support svg+xml/webp — so a server should only publish https:// or data: PNG/JPEG (optionally SVG/WebP) icons.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, heading '`icons`'; schema.ts interfaces Icons, Icon (2025-11-25 and 2026-07-28)_
- **TL-15** (MAY, modern): "MCP servers **MAY** designate specific tool parameters to be mirrored into HTTP headers using an `x-mcp-header` extension property" (value = name part of `Mcp-Param-{name}`). If used, the value "**MUST NOT** be empty", "**MUST** match HTTP field-name token syntax (`1*tchar`)", "**MUST NOT** contain control characters", "**MUST** be case-insensitively unique among all `x-mcp-header` values in the `inputSchema`", "**MUST** only be applied to parameters with primitive types (integer, string, boolean). Parameters with type `number` are not permitted. Integer values **MUST** be within the safe range ... (−2^53+1 to 2^53−1)", "**MUST** only be applied to properties that are _statically reachable_ from the schema root" (chain of `properties` keys only; not through items/oneOf/anyOf/allOf/not/if/then/else/$ref). Clients "**MUST** reject tool definitions where any `x-mcp-header` value violates these constraints" (tool excluded from tools/list). "Server developers **SHOULD NOT** mark sensitive parameters ... with `x-mcp-header`".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'x-mcp-header'; docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx heading 'Schema Extension'_
- **TL-16** (MUST, modern): On Streamable HTTP, `Mcp-Method` (=method) and `Mcp-Name` (=params.name for tools/call) "are **REQUIRED** for compliance". "Servers **MUST** reject requests with a recognized `Mcp-Param-{Name}` header that contains invalid characters"; "Any server that processes the message body **MUST** validate that encoded header values, after decoding if Base64-encoded, match the corresponding values in the request body. Servers **MUST** reject requests with a `400 Bad Request` HTTP status and JSON-RPC error code `-32020` (`HeaderMismatch`) if any validation fails." Table: value provided → "Server MUST validate header matches body"; value `null` or parameter absent → "Server MUST NOT expect the header"; client omits header but value in body → "Server MUST reject the request". Servers "**MUST** decode an encoded `Mcp-Name` or `Mcp-Param-{Name}` value" (`=?base64?...?=` sentinel) before comparing; integers "**SHOULD** compare ... numerically".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx, headings 'Standard Request Headers', 'Server Behavior for Custom Headers', 'Server Validation', 'Value Encoding'_
- **TL-17** (MUST, both): A tools/call result MUST contain `content` (array of content blocks, may be empty). Block shapes: text `{type:"text", text}`; image `{type:"image", data (base64), mimeType}`; audio `{type:"audio", data (base64), mimeType}`; resource_link `{type:"resource_link", uri, name, title?, description?, mimeType?, size?, icons?}`; embedded `{type:"resource", resource:{uri, mimeType?, text | blob}}`. "All content types ... support optional annotations" `{audience?: ("user"|"assistant")[], priority?: 0..1, lastModified?: ISO 8601}` and `_meta`. "A tool **MAY** return links to Resources"; "Resources **MAY** be embedded". Note: 2025-03-26 ContentBlock union has no `resource_link`.  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts interface CallToolResult (`content: ContentBlock[]` non-optional; schema.json required=[content,resultType]; 2025-06-18/2025-11-25 required=[content]); docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx heading 'Tool Result' and sub-headings 'Text Content', 'Image Content', 'Audio Content', 'Resource Links', 'Embedded Resources'_
- **TL-18** (MAY, modern): Modern: "**Structured** content is returned as a JSON value in the `structuredContent` field of a result. This can be any JSON value (object, array, string, number, boolean, or null) that conforms to the tool's `outputSchema` if one is defined." Legacy (2025-06-18, 2025-11-25): structuredContent MUST be a JSON object; 2025-03-26 has no structuredContent at all.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Structured Content'; schema.ts CallToolResult.structuredContent?: unknown. Legacy: 2025-06-18/2025-11-25 tools.mdx 'Structured Content' ("returned as a JSON object") and schema.ts structuredContent?: { [key: string]: unknown }_
- **TL-19** (MUST, both): "If an output schema is provided: Servers **MUST** provide structured results that conform to this schema. Clients **SHOULD** validate structured results against this schema." (i.e. when a tool advertises `outputSchema`, every successful result must carry a `structuredContent` that validates against it.)  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Output Schema' (identical in 2025-11-25 and 2025-06-18)_
- **TL-20** (SHOULD, both): "For backwards compatibility, a tool that returns structured content SHOULD also return the serialized JSON in a TextContent block." SEP-2106 strengthens this for non-object values: "servers using array or primitive `structuredContent` MUST also emit a `TextContent` block containing the serialized JSON".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Structured Content' (same in 2025-06-18/2025-11-25); seps/2106-json-schema-2020-12.md 'Backward Compatibility'_
- **TL-21** (SHOULD, both): "**Protocol Errors** indicate issues with the request structure itself ...: Unknown tool; Malformed requests (requests that fail to satisfy CallToolRequest schema); Server errors. They are returned as standard JSON-RPC errors" e.g. `{"code": -32602, "message": "Unknown tool: invalid_tool_name"}`. "**Tool Execution Errors** ...: API failures; Input validation errors (e.g., date in wrong format, value out of range); Business logic errors. They are reported in tool results with `isError: true`". schema.ts: "Any errors that originate from the tool SHOULD be reported inside the result object, with `isError` set to true, _not_ as an MCP protocol-level error response ... However, any errors in _finding_ the tool, an error indicating that the server does not support tool calls, or any other exceptional conditions, should be reported as an MCP error response." `isError` "If not set, this is assumed to be false".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Error Handling' (same in 2025-11-25; 2025-06-18/2025-03-26 listed 'Invalid arguments' under protocol errors, clarified by seps/1303-input-validation-errors-as-tool-execution-errors.md as backward compatible); schema.ts CallToolResult.isError doc_
- **TL-22** (MUST, both): "Servers **MUST**: Validate all tool inputs; Implement proper access controls; Rate limit tool invocations; Sanitize tool outputs".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Security Considerations' (identical in 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25)_
- **TL-23** (MAY, modern): "Servers **MAY** respond to `tools/call` with an `InputRequiredResult`" (`resultType: "input_required"`, `inputRequests?`, `requestState?`). If used: "`inputRequests` keys ... **MUST** be unique within the scope of the request"; values "**MUST** be one of ElicitRequest, CreateMessageRequest, or ListRootsRequest"; "servers **MUST** treat `requestState` as an attacker-controlled input. If `requestState` influences authorization, resource access, or business logic, servers **MUST** protect its integrity (e.g. HMAC or AEAD) and **MUST** reject state that fails verification"; "**SHOULD** include ... the authenticated principal ... a short expiry (TTL) ... an identifier for the originating request"; "Servers **MUST** include at least one of `inputRequests` or `requestState` in every `InputRequiredResult`"; "Servers **MUST NOT** send an `inputRequests` that the client has not declared support for in its capabilities"; "Servers **MUST NOT** assume that clients will fulfill the `inputRequests` or retry"; "Servers **MUST NOT** send `InputRequiredResult` responses on any other client requests" (only prompts/get, resources/read, tools/call); "Servers **SHOULD** validate that the data provided by the client is a valid `InputResponses` object"; unexpected parameters "**SHOULD** ignore"; missing info → "**SHOULD** respond with a new `InputRequiredResult`". Legacy era has no MRTR (server-initiated requests instead); a dual-era server must not emit `input_required` to legacy clients.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Input Required Tool Results'; docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/mrtr.mdx headings 'Supported Requests', 'Server Requirements (Basic Workflow)', 'Error Handling', 'Security Considerations'; schema.ts InputRequiredResult, InputResponseRequestParams (CallToolRequestParams extends it: inputResponses?, requestState?)_
- **TL-24** (MAY, both): A tools/call may carry `params._meta.progressToken` (string or integer). "The server **MAY** then send progress notifications" (`notifications/progress` with progressToken, progress, total?, message?). "Progress notifications **MUST** only reference tokens that: Were provided in an active request; Are associated with an in-progress operation"; "The `progress` value **MUST** increase with each notification"; "Progress notifications **MUST** stop after completion"; both parties "**SHOULD** implement rate limiting". Servers "**MAY**: Choose not to send any progress notifications".

- **TL-25** (SHOULD, modern): "Servers **SHOULD** include the following `io.modelcontextprotocol/*` field in every result's `_meta`, unless specifically configured not to do so ...: `io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo` (Implementation)". Applies to tools/list and tools/call results (cross-cutting with the lifecycle area). Implementations "**MUST NOT** make assumptions about values" at reserved `_meta` keys; tool-supplied `_meta` must be merged, not overwritten.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, heading '`_meta`', sub-section 'Per-response protocol fields'; schema.ts ResultMetaObject_
- **TL-26** (SHOULD, both): "When the list of available tools changes, servers that declared the `listChanged` capability **SHOULD** send a notification" `notifications/tools/list_changed` — modern: only "to clients that have opened a `subscriptions/listen` stream with `toolsListChanged: true`". Not required when `listChanged` is not declared.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'List Changed Notification' (legacy: 2025-11-25 same heading, sent on the server→client channel)_
- **TL-27** (SHOULD, both): "Servers that use embedded resources **SHOULD** implement the `resources` capability".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/tools.mdx, heading 'Embedded Resources'_
- **TL-28** (MUST NOT, modern): When mapping tool/protocol failures to JSON-RPC codes: "Implementations **MUST NOT** emit any code from this sub-range [-32020..-32099] that is not defined by this specification and **MUST** use defined codes only with their specified meanings"; "New codes **MUST NOT** be allocated in [-32000..-32019]"; "**MUST NOT** emit ... `-32002` ... `-32042`". Defined: -32020 HeaderMismatch, -32021 MissingRequiredClientCapability, -32022 UnsupportedProtocolVersion; standard -32700/-32600..-32603.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx, heading 'Error Codes'_
- **TL-29** (MUST, both): Derived from the schema: `params.name` is a required string; `params.arguments` is OPTIONAL. A `tools/call` without `arguments` (e.g. for a zero-parameter tool) satisfies the CallToolRequest schema and MUST be processed normally, not treated as malformed and not allowed to crash. A request whose `arguments` is present but not an object, or whose `name` is missing/not a string, fails the schema and is a protocol error (-32602).  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts interface CallToolRequestParams (`arguments?: { [key: string]: unknown }`; identical optionality in 2025-03-26/2025-06-18/2025-11-25); tools.mdx 'Error Handling' (only requests failing the CallToolRequest schema are protocol errors)_

## Resources, resource templates, prompts, caching, pagination, completion (server side; dual-era 2026-07-28 modern + 2025-03-26/2025-06-18/2025-11-25 legacy)

Requirements extracted: 49. Gaps: 17.


### GAP-RS-01 [blocker, effort S] No `resultType` on any resources (or other) result

- Requirements: RS-18, RS-05, RS-07, RT-01, PR-03, PR-04, CO-02
- Current: ListResources builds {resources:[...]} only (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:102-117); ReadResource builds {contents:[...]} (:142-172); ListResourceTemplates builds {resourceTemplates:[]} (:186-193). TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest wraps whatever the manager returns without adding fields (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:192-200).
- Required: Modern: "The `result` MUST include a `resultType` field" — "complete" on every ordinary result, "input_required" on MRTR interim results.
- Compat impact: Legacy clients ignore unknown result fields (Result has [key:string]:unknown in all revisions) so emitting it unconditionally is harmless, but emit only for modern-era requests to stay schema-exact. Library consumers: if injected centrally in the processor, custom IMCPCapabilityManager implementations get it for free with no code change; managers that deliberately return an input_required result must be able to set it themselves (processor must not overwrite an existing key).
- Suggested change: In MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas, after ExecuteMethodCall and when the request is modern (per-request `_meta` present / negotiated version >= 2026-07-28 from the request-context object the lifecycle work introduces): if ExecuteResult is a TJSONObject and GetValue('resultType') = nil then AddPair('resultType','complete'). Add const MCP_RESULT_TYPE_COMPLETE = 'complete' / MCP_RESULT_TYPE_INPUT_REQUIRED = 'input_required' in MCPServer.Types.pas. Do not add it inside each manager (avoids duplicating it in ToolsManager/ResourcesManager/PromptsManager and covers third-party managers).

### GAP-CA-01 [blocker, effort M] `ttlMs` and `cacheScope` missing on resources/list, resources/read, resources/templates/list

- Requirements: CA-01, CA-02, CA-03, CA-04, CA-05, CA-06
- Current: None of the three result builders emit ttlMs/cacheScope (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:102-117, :142-172, :186-193). IMCPResource exposes only URI/Name/Description/MimeType/Read (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:11-23), so there is no per-resource place to declare freshness or scope.
- Required: Modern: "Servers MUST include caching hints on results with `resultType: "complete"` returned by ... `prompts/list`, `resources/list`, `resources/templates/list`, `resources/read`"; "Servers MUST provide a `ttlMs` value that is `>= 0`"; cacheScope required, "public"|"private"; same cacheScope on all pages.
- Compat impact: Legacy clients: extra fields are tolerated (Result allows additional keys); emit only in modern era for schema exactness. Library consumers: do NOT add methods to IMCPResource (custom classes implementing the interface directly would stop compiling); add an additive interface and default fields in TMCPResourceBase<T> so existing descendants compile unchanged and get conservative defaults (ttlMs 0, cacheScope 'private').
- Suggested change: MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas: add `TMCPCacheScope = (csPublic, csPrivate)` and `IMCPCacheableResource = interface ['{new guid}'] function GetTtlMs: Integer; function GetCacheScope: TMCPCacheScope; end;` implemented by TMCPResourceBase<T> via new protected fields FTtlMs: Integer (default 0) and FCacheScope: TMCPCacheScope (default csPrivate) that descendants set in Create. TMCPResourcesManager: add published properties ListTtlMs: Integer (default 0), ListCacheScope: TMCPCacheScope (default csPrivate; the sample dpr sets csPublic + 300000 because the set is fixed at construction and there is no auth), used for resources/list and resources/templates/list; in ReadResource query `Supports(Resource, IMCPCacheableResource, Cacheable)` and fall back to (0, csPrivate). Emit `ttlMs` (TJSONNumber) and `cacheScope` ('public'|'private') only for modern-era requests. Sample values: project://info and project://readme -> FTtlMs := 3600000, FCacheScope := csPublic (static text); logs://recent and server://status -> FTtlMs := 0, FCacheScope := csPrivate (dynamic process telemetry; logs://recent even mutates the buffer on read at /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Logs.pas:209). Document in README that a deployment adding authorization must set csPrivate on anything user-dependent (CA-05).

### GAP-RS-02 [blocker, effort M] Resource not found is returned as a successful text result instead of a JSON-RPC error (-32602 modern / -32002 legacy)

- Requirements: RS-10, RS-11, RS-12, RS-13, XC-05
- Current: ReadResource on unknown URI returns HTTP 200 result {contents:[{uri, mimeType:'text/plain', text:'Error: Resource not found: <uri>'}]} (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:165-170). CreateErrorResponse cannot carry `data` (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:128-144). Trap: ProcessRequest maps ANY exception whose message contains 'not found' to -32601 METHOD_NOT_FOUND (:214-215), so simply raising Exception('Resource not found') would yield the wrong code (and, once the transport maps -32601 to HTTP 404 in modern, the wrong HTTP status too).
- Required: Modern: "servers MUST return a JSON-RPC error with code `-32602` (Invalid Params)" with data.uri (SHOULD, SEP-2164) and "MUST NOT emit" -32002. Legacy (2025-03-26/06-18/11-25): "Resource not found: `-32002`" (SHOULD). Never an empty contents array.
- Compat impact: Legacy clients that today show the 'Error: Resource not found' text will instead receive a JSON-RPC error (-32002) — a behaviour change, but the one the legacy spec asks for and what every official SDK does (SEP-2164 table). Library consumers: custom resources unchanged; custom managers can adopt the new typed exception but are not forced to (generic Exception still maps to -32603).
- Suggested change: New unit src/Protocol/MCPServer.Errors.pas: `EMCPError = class(Exception) Code: Integer; Data: TJSONValue (owned); constructor Create(ACode: Integer; const AMessage: string; AData: TJSONValue = nil)`; constants MCP_ERR_RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND_LEGACY = -32002 in MCPServer.Types (kept only for legacy era). In TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest add `on E: EMCPError do Result := CreateErrorResponse(id, E.Code, E.Message, E.Data)` BEFORE the generic handler and remove/limit the substring 'not found' heuristic to the processor's own 'Method [..] not found' exception (better: raise EMCPError(-32601) from ExecuteMethodCall). In TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource: `if not FResources.TryGetValue(URI, Resource) then raise EMCPError.Create(IfThen(Ctx.IsModern, JSONRPC_INVALID_PARAMS, MCP_ERR_RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND_LEGACY), 'Resource not found', TJSONObject.Create.AddPair('uri', URI))`. The era comes from the shared request context (thread-local `TMCPRequestContext.Current` or an optional `IMCPCapabilityManagerEx.ExecuteMethodEx(Method, Params, Context)` discovered with Supports(), leaving IMCPCapabilityManager.ExecuteMethod intact for third-party managers). Keep HTTP status 200 for this application-level -32602 (only header/_meta failures mandate 400; a 400 with a non-modern error body would trigger dual-era clients' legacy fallback).

### GAP-RS-03 [major, effort S] Resource read exceptions are swallowed into the `text` field instead of a -32603 error

- Requirements: RS-10, RS-13, PR-07
- Current: ReadResource catches any exception from Resource.Read and returns a success result whose text is 'Error reading resource: ' + E.Message (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:155-163).
- Required: "Servers SHOULD return `-32603` for internal errors" (modern MUST-adjacent wording in the same paragraph as the -32602 MUST); legacy "Internal errors: `-32603`".
- Compat impact: Behaviour change for both eras: a failing custom TMCPResourceBase<T>.GetResourceData now produces a JSON-RPC error instead of a text blob (clients will finally see failures as failures). No compile impact.
- Suggested change: Remove the try/except at ResourcesManager.pas:155-163; let EMCPError propagate as-is and wrap any other Exception as EMCPError.Create(JSONRPC_INTERNAL_ERROR, E.Message). Log at Error level with TLogger before re-raising.

### GAP-RS-04 [major, effort S] Missing/invalid `uri` param is not rejected with -32602 (nil params crashes)

- Requirements: RS-07, RS-14, XC-03
- Current: ReadResource calls Params.GetValue('uri') without checking Params (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:134-138); the processor passes Params = nil when the request has no params object (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:185-188) -> EAccessViolation -> -32603 'Access violation ...'. An empty or non-string uri falls into the 'not found' text path. No URI syntax validation exists anywhere (registry keys are arbitrary strings, /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Core/MCPServer.Registration.pas:59-65).
- Required: `uri` is a required param (ResourceRequestParams); "Servers MUST validate all resource URIs"; malformed params -> -32602.
- Compat impact: None for clients that send valid requests. Library consumers: registering a resource whose URI has no RFC3986 scheme would now fail fast at TMCPRegistry.RegisterResource (surface as an exception at startup) — document.
- Suggested change: In ReadResource: `if (Params = nil) or not (Params.GetValue('uri') is TJSONString) or (URI = '') then raise EMCPError.Create(JSONRPC_INVALID_PARAMS, 'Missing required parameter: uri')`. Add `class function TMCPUriUtils.IsValidUri(const S: string): Boolean` (scheme = ^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*: via System.RegularExpressions, no control chars/whitespace) in a small src/Protocol/MCPServer.Uri.pas; call it in TMCPRegistry.RegisterResource (raise EArgumentException) and in ReadResource (-32602 'Invalid resource URI').

### GAP-RS-05 [major, effort S] `resources/subscribe` / `resources/unsubscribe` handling and advertised `subscribe` flag per era

- Requirements: RS-17, RS-03, RS-02, XC-01
- Current: HandlesMethod does not include subscribe/unsubscribe (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:59-64) so they fall through to 'Method [...] not found' -> -32601 (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:121-123, :214-215) but always with HTTP 200 (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Server/MCPServer.IdHTTPServer.pas:509, :553). Capability advertised as {subscribe:false, listChanged:false} only in `initialize` (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:116-124); no subscriptions/listen support exists.
- Required: Modern: the methods no longer exist; unknown method -> -32601 and (HTTP) 404; subscriptions only via subscriptions/listen with resourceSubscriptions. Legacy: with subscribe:false the server need not implement them (-32601 acceptable). Advertising {subscribe:false, listChanged:false} (or {}) is valid in both eras.
- Compat impact: No client-visible change beyond the HTTP status in modern (transport area). Library consumers unaffected.
- Suggested change: Keep `resources: {subscribe: false, listChanged: false}` (or `{}`) in both `initialize` and `server/discover`. Do not add handlers for resources/subscribe|unsubscribe. Ensure the transport maps -32601 to HTTP 404 for modern-era requests (cross-reference transport/lifecycle work item; the JsonRpcProcessor should expose the error code to TMCPIdHTTPServer, e.g. ProcessRequest returning a record {Body, ErrorCode} or an out parameter). If listChanged/subscribe are ever enabled, a `TMCPSubscriptionsManager` (subscriptions/listen) is the modern delivery path — out of scope here.

### GAP-RS-06 [major, effort M] Capabilities are hard-coded in CoreManager rather than derived from the registered managers

- Requirements: RS-01, RS-04, PR-01, CO-01
- Current: TMCPCoreManager.Initialize always emits `tools` and `resources` capabilities (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:106-124) regardless of whether TMCPToolsManager/TMCPResourcesManager were registered (README 'Using as a Library' lets consumers register any subset; /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/README.md lines ~200-215). A consumer that omits TMCPResourcesManager still advertises `resources`, and a future PromptsManager/CompletionManager would have to edit CoreManager to be advertised.
- Required: "Servers that declare the `resources` capability MUST respond to `resources/list`"; "Servers that support prompts MUST declare the `prompts` capability"; "Servers that support completions MUST declare the `completions` capability". Both eras (initialize result / DiscoverResult).
- Compat impact: Legacy clients see the same capability JSON for the default configuration. Library consumers: custom managers that implement the new optional interface become advertisable; those that do not are simply not listed (today they are never listed either), so no break.
- Suggested change: MCPServer.Types.pas: add `IMCPCapabilityProvider = interface ['{new guid}'] function GetCapabilityJSON: TJSONObject; end;` (returns the value object, e.g. {} or {listChanged:false}). Implement in TMCPToolsManager ({listChanged:false}), TMCPResourcesManager ({subscribe:false, listChanged:false}), TMCPPromptsManager ({listChanged:false}), TMCPCompletionManager ({}). Extend IMCPManagerRegistry with `function GetManagers: TArray<IMCPCapabilityManager>` (additive) and let TMCPCoreManager (initialize) and the new server/discover handler build `capabilities` by iterating managers and Supports(Manager, IMCPCapabilityProvider). Remove the unused TMCPCapabilities/TMCPToolsCapability/'supportsProgress'/'supportsCancellation' keys (not in any revision's ServerCapabilities schema) or keep them only under `experimental`.

### GAP-RS-08 [major, effort M] Binary (blob) resource contents and multi-part contents are impossible through IMCPResource

- Requirements: RS-07, RS-08, PR-05
- Current: IMCPResource.Read returns a single string (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:17, :91-134); ReadResource always writes one {uri, mimeType, text} item (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:147-157). TResourceContent (Base.pas:43-52) has only Text and is unused.
- Required: "Resources can contain either text or binary data": BlobResourceContents {uri, mimeType?, blob: base64}; "Binary data MUST be properly encoded"; "Servers MAY return multiple resource contents in response to a single `resources/read` request."
- Compat impact: Purely additive: IMCPResource and TMCPResourceBase<T>.Read keep working (text path). Existing custom resources need no change. The same content record is reusable for prompt embedded resources.
- Suggested change: MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas: `TMCPResourceContents = record URI, MimeType: string; Text: string; Blob: TBytes; IsBlob: Boolean; Meta: TJSONObject; class function FromText(...)/FromBytes(...) end;` and additive interface `IMCPResourceReader = interface ['{new guid}'] function ReadContents: TArray<TMCPResourceContents>; end;`. TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource: if Supports(Resource, IMCPResourceReader) use it, else wrap Resource.Read into one text item. Serialize blob with `TBase64Encoding.Create(0).EncodeBytesToString(Bytes)` (line-length 0 -> no CR/LF; the default TNetEncoding.Base64 inserts line breaks every 76 chars, which would corrupt `blob`). Provide `TMCPBinaryResourceBase = class(TInterfacedObject, IMCPResource, IMCPResourceReader)` with abstract `function GetBytes(out MimeType: string): TBytes` and a sample (e.g. server icon PNG) to prove the path; set `size` = Length(Bytes) when known.

### GAP-XC-01 [major, effort S] Mcp-Name header (params.uri / params.name) not validated for resources/read and prompts/get

- Requirements: XC-04
- Current: TMCPIdHTTPServer reads only Accept, Origin and Mcp-Session-Id (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Server/MCPServer.IdHTTPServer.pas:277, :224, :333); no Mcp-Method/Mcp-Name handling.
- Required: Modern HTTP: `Mcp-Name` REQUIRED for resources/read (params.uri) and prompts/get (params.name); servers processing the body "MUST reject requests where the values specified in the headers do not match" with 400 + -32020, decoding `=?base64?…?=` values.
- Compat impact: Transport-layer concern (cross-reference the Streamable HTTP work item); legacy sessions must not be subjected to this check. Managers only need the body value, so no manager/library change.
- Suggested change: In the transport's modern-request validation step (owned by the transport work item) add: for method in ['resources/read','prompts/get','tools/call'] compare decoded Mcp-Name with params.uri/params.name (case-sensitive) and Mcp-Method with method; on mismatch respond 400 with EMCPError(-32020,'HeaderMismatch', data {header, expected}). Resource URIs with non-token characters will arrive Base64-sentinel encoded; decode with TBase64Encoding before comparing.

### GAP-RS-07 [minor, effort S] Resource descriptor supports only uri/name/description/mimeType; optional fields (title, size, icons, annotations, _meta) unsupported and empty strings are emitted

- Requirements: RS-06, RT-01, PR-03
- Current: ListResources emits exactly uri, name, description, mimeType (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:109-114) even when Description/MimeType are '' (ReadResource likewise emits mimeType '' at :153). IMCPResource / TMCPResourceBase<T> have no Title/Size/Icons/Annotations/Meta (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:11-41).
- Required: Resource: `mimeType` "if known" (optional), `title`?, `size`?, `icons`?, `annotations`?, `_meta`? — all optional but part of the data model in both eras (title 2025-06-18+, icons 2025-11-25+).
- Compat impact: Omitting empty mimeType/description is safe for all clients. New fields are optional; 2025-03-26 JSON schema does not set additionalProperties:false, so gating by negotiated version is polish, not a hard requirement. Library consumers: additive protected fields on TMCPResourceBase<T>; no signature changes.
- Suggested change: Add to TMCPResourceBase<T>: FTitle: string; FSize: Int64 (-1 = unknown); FAnnotations: TJSONObject (nil = none; audience/priority/lastModified); FIcons: TJSONArray (nil = none); FMeta: TJSONObject; expose through an additive `IMCPResourceMetadata` interface (GetTitle, GetSize, GetAnnotations, GetIcons, GetMeta). Add a shared `TMCPResourceJson.Describe(const R: IMCPResource; const Ctx): TJSONObject` helper used by ListResources, resource_link content blocks and (later) templates that adds optional pairs only when non-empty/-1/nil; gate `title`/`_meta` to negotiated version >= 2025-06-18 and `icons` to >= 2025-11-25 (modern always).

### GAP-PG-01 [minor, effort S] Pagination cursors ignored; invalid cursors accepted; list order non-deterministic

- Requirements: PG-01, PG-02, PG-03, PG-04, PG-05, CA-04
- Current: ExecuteMethod calls ListResources/ListResourceTemplates without passing Params, so `cursor` is never read (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:66-76, :93-122); any cursor value silently yields the full list again. Lists iterate TDictionary.Values (:107) whose order is unspecified (same in ToolsManager :223).
- Required: "Invalid cursors SHOULD result in an error with code -32602"; "Servers SHOULD: Provide stable cursors"; end-of-list signalled by omitting nextCursor (never ""); same cacheScope across pages; deterministic ordering recommended for caching.
- Compat impact: Behaviour change only for clients that send a cursor the server never issued (they now get -32602 instead of a duplicate full page) — spec-endorsed. Default page size 'unlimited' keeps single-page behaviour for all existing clients. Library consumers: additive helper; custom managers may adopt it.
- Suggested change: New src/Protocol/MCPServer.Pagination.pas: `TMCPPagination` with `class function ParseCursor(const Params: TJSONObject; out Offset: Integer): Boolean` (cursor = Base64Url of 'v1:' + offset; any absent-key -> offset 0; any unparsable/out-of-range value incl. '' -> raise EMCPError(-32602,'Invalid cursor')) and `class function Slice(Total, Offset, PageSize: Integer; out NextCursor: string): Integer` (NextCursor := '' meaning omit when Offset+PageSize >= Total). Add `PageSize: Integer` property (0 = no pagination) to TMCPResourcesManager (and TMCPToolsManager/TMCPPromptsManager). Replace TDictionary iteration with a sorted TList<string> of keys (sort by URI/name) or preserve registration order to make cursors stable. Pass Params into ListResources/ListResourceTemplates (private signature change only).

### GAP-XC-02 [minor, effort S] `_meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo` not included on results

- Requirements: XC-02
- Current: serverInfo is only emitted inside the initialize result (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:128-131); no result carries `_meta`.
- Required: Modern: "Servers SHOULD include ... `io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo` in every result's `_meta`, unless specifically configured not to do so".
- Compat impact: Extra `_meta` on legacy results is schema-valid (Result._meta exists in every revision) but gate to modern era anyway. No library impact if injected centrally next to resultType.
- Suggested change: Same injection point as GAP-RS-01 in TMCPJsonRpcProcessor: for modern requests ensure result._meta exists and add 'io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo' = {name: Settings.ServerName, version: Settings.ServerVersion} when absent; make it switchable via a TMCPSettings.EmitServerInfo boolean (default True). Requires the processor to receive TMCPSettings (constructor overload; keep existing constructor for compatibility).

### GAP-RS-10 [minor, effort S] project://info resource hard-codes 'MCP ' + MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION and README/features advertise 2025-06-18 only

- Requirements: RS-06, XC-05
- Current: TProjectInfoResource reports Protocol := 'MCP ' + MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Project.pas:101) with MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION = '2025-06-18' (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:11); README badge/features say 2025-06-18 and 'four essential resources' (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/README.md 'Features', 'Available Example resources').
- Required: Informational only, but the dual-era server will support several revisions; resource text and docs must not misstate them.
- Compat impact: None.
- Suggested change: Replace with the supported-versions list the lifecycle work introduces (e.g. String.Join(', ', MCP_SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS)); add 'Prompts capability' / 'Resource templates' / 'Completion' to Features when implemented; update README sections 'Features', 'Available Example resources', add 'Available Example prompts' and a 'Creating Custom Prompts' snippet mirroring 'Creating Custom Resources'.

### GAP-RT-01 [optional, effort L] Resource templates are not supported (registry is exact-URI only; templates list always empty)

- Requirements: RT-01, RT-02, RS-14
- Current: TMCPRegistry stores factories keyed by literal URI (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Core/MCPServer.Registration.pas:21, :59-65, :79-89); ReadResource does an exact TryGetValue (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:150); ListResourceTemplates hard-codes an empty array with the comment 'this server doesn't support resource templates' (:186-193).
- Required: Optional feature: `resourceTemplates` entries with `uriTemplate` (RFC 6570), `name`, optional title/description/mimeType/icons/annotations; reads of URIs matching a template must resolve; templates MAY be completed via completion/complete (ref/resource).
- Compat impact: Additive. Existing exact-URI registration path untouched; resources/templates/list keeps returning [] until a template is registered. Both eras use the same wire shape (plus modern caching fields).
- Suggested change: MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas: `TMCPTemplateVars = TDictionary<string,string>`; `IMCPResourceTemplate = interface ['{new guid}'] function GetUriTemplate: string; GetName; GetTitle; GetDescription; GetMimeType; function Matches(const URI: string; Vars: TMCPTemplateVars): Boolean; function CreateResource(const URI: string; Vars: TMCPTemplateVars): IMCPResource; end;` plus `TMCPResourceTemplateBase` implementing RFC 6570 level 1/2 matching (`{var}` -> `([^/]+)`, `{+var}` -> `(.+)`, `{/var}`, `{?var}` optional) by compiling the template to a System.RegularExpressions.TRegEx with named groups, percent-decoding captured values. MCPServer.Registration.pas: `TMCPResourceTemplateFactory = reference to function: IMCPResourceTemplate; class procedure RegisterResourceTemplate(const UriTemplate: string; Factory)`; `GetResourceTemplateURIs`; `CreateResourceTemplate`. TMCPResourcesManager: FTemplates: TList<IMCPResourceTemplate>; ListResourceTemplates emits them (with pagination/caching fields); ReadResource: exact lookup first, then first template whose Matches succeeds; else not-found error. Sample: `logs://{level}` returning filtered logs via TLogBuffer.GetLogs(100, Level) (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Logs.pas:160).

### GAP-PR-01 [optional, effort L] Prompts capability entirely missing (no prompts/list, prompts/get, no prompt abstraction)

- Requirements: PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-04, PR-05, PR-06, PR-07, PR-08, PR-09, CA-01, PG-01, RS-18
- Current: No unit handles 'prompts/list' or 'prompts/get'; TMCPRegistry knows only tools and resources (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Core/MCPServer.Registration.pas:13-36); CoreManager never advertises `prompts` (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:103-124); MCPServer.dpr registers only Core/Tools/Resources managers (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/MCPServer.dpr:84-91, :125-132). A client sending prompts/list gets -32601 via the 'not found' substring path (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:214), which is the correct answer for a server that does not offer prompts.
- Required: Prompts are a MAY feature, but once offered: capability declared; prompts/list -> {prompts[], nextCursor?, (modern) resultType, ttlMs, cacheScope}; prompts/get -> {description?, messages[{role, content}], (modern) resultType}; content blocks text/image/audio/resource_link/resource; -32602 for unknown prompt or missing required argument; -32603 internal; MRTR (modern MAY); listChanged notifications only if advertised.
- Compat impact: Fully additive: new units, new TMCPRegistry methods, new manager registered in the dpr and documented in README. Existing tools/resources code and consumer code untouched. Legacy clients (2025-03-26) must not receive `resource_link` content, `title` or `icons` — gate by negotiated version. prompts/get result must NOT carry ttlMs/cacheScope (not a CacheableResult).
- Suggested change: New unit src/Prompts/MCPServer.Prompt.Base.pas: `TMCPPromptArgument = record Name, Title, Description: string; Required: Boolean end;` `TMCPPromptMessages = class` with fluent helpers AddText(Role, Text), AddImage(Role, Bytes, MimeType), AddAudio(...), AddResourceLink(Role, const R: IMCPResource), AddEmbeddedResource(Role, const C: TMCPResourceContents), each producing the ContentBlock JSON (base64 via TBase64Encoding.Create(0)); `IMCPPrompt = interface ['{new guid}'] function GetName/GetTitle/GetDescription: string; function GetArguments: TArray<TMCPPromptArgument>; function Get(const Arguments: TJSONObject; Messages: TMCPPromptMessages): string {returns result description}; end;` `TMCPPromptBase = class(TInterfacedObject, IMCPPrompt)` (FName/FTitle/FDescription/FArguments; abstract BuildMessages) and generic `TMCPPromptBase<T: class, constructor>` mirroring TMCPToolBase<T> (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Base.pas:44-60): arguments derived from T's writable string properties via RTTI reusing [SchemaDescription]/[Optional] attributes (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:14-23) -> name = LowerCase(prop), required = not Optional; Get deserializes `arguments` with TMCPSerializer.Deserialize<T> (map its EArgumentException 'Unknown parameter' to -32602) and checks every non-Optional property is present in the JSON (-32602 'Missing required argument: x'); then `ExecuteWithParams(const Params: T; Messages: TMCPPromptMessages): string; virtual; abstract`. MCPServer.Registration.pas: `TMCPPromptFactory = reference to function: IMCPPrompt; RegisterPrompt(Name, Factory); CreatePrompt; GetPromptNames; HasPrompt` (+ FPrompts dictionary freed in finalization). New src/Managers/MCPServer.PromptsManager.pas: `TMCPPromptsManager = class(TInterfacedObject, IMCPCapabilityManager, IMCPCapabilityProvider)`; GetCapabilityName='prompts'; HandlesMethod 'prompts/list','prompts/get'; ListPrompts (deterministic order by name, shared pagination helper, modern ttlMs/cacheScope from ListTtlMs/ListCacheScope properties, optional fields emitted only when set, `title`/`icons` gated by version); GetPrompt: validate params/name (-32602 'Prompt not found', data {name}), `arguments` must be an object whose values are strings (-32602), catch EMCPInputRequired -> {resultType:'input_required', inputRequests?, requestState?} in modern era (in legacy era convert to -32603 'Interactive input not supported by client'), other exceptions -> -32603. Register in MCPServer.dpr (both RunHTTPServer and RunStdioServer) and README 'Using as a Library'. Sample src/Prompts/MCPServer.Prompt.SummarizeLogs.pas: argument `level` (Optional), returns one user text message plus an embedded `logs://recent` resource (demonstrates embedded resources).

### GAP-CO-01 [optional, effort M] completion/complete not implemented and `completions` capability not advertised

- Requirements: CO-01, CO-02, CO-03, CO-04, RT-02
- Current: No manager handles 'completion/complete'; capability never emitted (/home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:103-124). Requests currently get -32601 (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:121-123, :214) — correct for a server without the capability.
- Required: Optional: if offered, advertise {"completions": {}}; validate ref (ref/prompt needs name, ref/resource needs uri) and argument {name,value}; return {completion:{values (<=100), total?, hasMore?}} (+ modern resultType, no caching fields); -32602 for unknown prompt/template; MUST validate inputs, rate-limit, prevent information disclosure.
- Compat impact: Additive. Only advertised when TMCPCompletionManager is registered, so existing deployments are unchanged. Legacy 2025-03-26 clients do not send `context` — treat as optional.
- Suggested change: Additive interface in MCPServer.Prompt.Base.pas / Resource.Base.pas: `TMCPCompletion = record Values: TArray<string>; Total: Integer (-1 unknown); HasMore: Boolean end; IMCPCompletable = interface ['{new guid}'] function Complete(const ArgumentName, Value: string; const Context: TDictionary<string,string>): TMCPCompletion; end;` implemented optionally by prompts and resource templates. New src/Managers/MCPServer.CompletionManager.pas: `TMCPCompletionManager(APrompts: TMCPPromptsManager; AResources: TMCPResourcesManager)`; HandlesMethod 'completion/complete'; parse params.ref.type ('ref/prompt' -> TMCPRegistry/PromptsManager lookup by name; 'ref/resource' -> template by uriTemplate, falling back to exact resource) else -32602; missing argument.name/value -> -32602; if target not IMCPCompletable return {values:[], hasMore:false}; truncate values to 100 and set hasMore; capability JSON {} via IMCPCapabilityProvider. Add a simple per-client token bucket (rate limit) hook or document that rate limiting is left to the host.

### GAP-RS-09 [optional, effort M] No way for a resource or prompt to return an InputRequiredResult (MRTR)

- Requirements: RS-09, PR-06, RS-18
- Current: Managers can only return a TJSONObject result or raise; no representation of input_required/inputRequests/requestState exists (IMCPResource.Read: string at /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:17; ReadResource builds only contents at ResourcesManager.pas:142-172).
- Required: Modern MAY: resources/read and prompts/get MAY answer with {resultType:'input_required', inputRequests?, requestState?}; MUST include at least one of the two; MUST NOT include inputRequests the client did not declare capability for (from `_meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities`); requestState is attacker-controlled and MUST be integrity-protected when it affects authorization; MUST NOT be sent on any other method.
- Compat impact: Additive. Only resources/prompts/tools that raise the new exception are affected; in legacy era the manager converts it to a -32603 error (legacy servers would instead have issued a server-initiated elicitation request, which this server never supported).
- Suggested change: Shared with tools/call: in MCPServer.Errors.pas add `EMCPInputRequired = class(Exception) InputRequests: TJSONObject (owned, may be nil); RequestState: string; end;` and `TMCPInputResponses` accessor on the request context (params.inputResponses / params.requestState). TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource and TMCPPromptsManager.GetPrompt catch EMCPInputRequired and (modern only) return {resultType:'input_required', inputRequests?, requestState?} WITHOUT ttlMs/cacheScope; verify each inputRequests[*].method against the client capabilities in the request context (elicitation/sampling/roots) and drop/raise -32021 MissingRequiredClientCapability otherwise. Provide `TMCPRequestState.Sign/Verify` helper (HMAC-SHA256 with a per-process secret, System.Hash.THashSHA2.GetHMAC) so consumers do not hand-roll integrity protection.

### Already conformant

- `resources` capability is declared (RS-01) with `subscribe: false, listChanged: false` in the initialize result — valid shape in every legacy revision and in 2026-07-28 ("may advertise ... neither"): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:116-124
- resources/list, resources/read and resources/templates/list are all dispatched by the resources manager (RS-05, RS-07, RT-01): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:59-76
- resources/list emits the two required Resource fields `uri` and `name` for every entry: /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:109-112
- resources/read result uses the correct TextResourceContents shape {uri, mimeType, text} inside a `contents` array (RS-07/RS-08): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:144-157; JSON resources are serialized as a JSON text string and text resources via the Content property: /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:99-130
- resources/templates/list returns a well-formed empty `resourceTemplates: []` (an empty set is explicitly allowed): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:186-193
- The resource set is fixed at manager construction from the registry, so it never varies per connection or as a side effect of other requests (RS-04): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:83-91
- Never returns an empty `contents` array for an unknown resource (RS-11) — it returns one text item (wrong per GAP-RS-02, but not the ambiguous empty array): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:147-170
- Never emits -32002 (RS-12, trivially, because no not-found error is emitted at all): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:27-32 defines only the five standard codes
