- Built-in URIs `project://info`, `project://readme`, `logs://recent`, `server://status` use RFC3986-syntactic custom schemes and avoid `https://` for server-served content (RS-14 custom schemes, RS-15): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Project.pas:87,116; .../MCPServer.Resource.Logs.pas:196; .../MCPServer.Resource.Server.pas:116-121
- Unknown methods (resources/subscribe, resources/unsubscribe, prompts/*, completion/complete) are answered with JSON-RPC -32601, which is the right answer for capabilities the server does not advertise (RS-03 legacy with subscribe:false, CO-03, PR capability negotiation): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:121-123, :214-215
- Not advertising `prompts` or `completions` while not implementing them respects capability negotiation (PR-01/CO-01 apply only to servers that support the feature): /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:103-124
- Registration API for library consumers (TMCPRegistry.RegisterResource + factory, TMCPResourceBase<T>) is a clean extension point that the suggested additive interfaces (IMCPCacheableResource, IMCPResourceReader, IMCPResourceMetadata, IMCPResourceTemplate, IMCPPrompt) can build on without changing IMCPResource: /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Core/MCPServer.Registration.pas:15-36; /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:11-41

### Notes

- Era selection is per request: a request with modern `_meta` is served under 2026-07-28 rules; an `initialize` handshake pins legacy rules for that HTTP session / stdio process (versioning.mdx § Backward Compatibility). The resources/prompts managers therefore need the negotiated revision and the client capabilities at execution time. Recommend ONE shared mechanism (thread-local `TMCPRequestContext.Current` set by TMCPJsonRpcProcessor, or an additive `IMCPCapabilityManagerEx.ExecuteMethodEx(Method, Params, Context)` discovered with Supports()) so IMCPCapabilityManager.ExecuteMethod keeps its signature for third-party managers.
- Error-code matrix for 'resource not found': modern MUST -32602 (+ data.uri SHOULD) and MUST NOT -32002; legacy 2025-03-26/06-18/11-25 SHOULD -32002. Clients of 2026-07-28 SHOULD still accept -32002, so a lazy single-code implementation would 'work', but the modern MUST NOT makes the dual-code switch mandatory for conformance.
- Trap in the current processor: any exception message containing 'not found' is turned into -32601 (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:214). Once the transport maps -32601 to HTTP 404 for modern requests (streamable-http.mdx), a naive 'Resource not found' exception would become a 404 that dual-era clients may misread as a legacy server. Introduce typed EMCPError before touching the managers.
- HTTP status for application-level -32602 (resource not found, invalid cursor, unknown prompt, missing prompt argument) should stay 200: the spec only mandates 400 for malformed `_meta`/header errors, and streamable-http § Backward Compatibility says clients fall back to `initialize` on a 4xx whose body is not a recognized modern error (-32020/-32021/-32022).
- ttlMs/cacheScope are only required in the modern era; they are not part of any legacy Result schema. Since legacy JSON schemas do not set additionalProperties:false they would be tolerated, but gating them (like resultType) keeps legacy responses byte-identical to today's, which is the safest compatibility story.
- Recommended cache values for the built-ins: project://info and project://readme -> ttlMs 3600000, cacheScope public (static strings); logs://recent -> ttlMs 0, private (dynamic, and GetResourceData mutates the buffer at Resource.Logs.pas:209 — consider dropping that access-log side effect); server://status -> ttlMs 0, private (live telemetry). resources/list and resources/templates/list -> public with e.g. 300000 in the sample server (set fixed at construction, no auth); library default should be (0, private) which can never be wrong (CA-05).
- GetPromptResult and CompleteResult are NOT CacheableResult — do not add ttlMs/cacheScope there. InputRequiredResult interim results also carry no caching hints.
- Pagination is optional; the simplest conformant behaviour is 'never emit nextCursor, reject any supplied cursor with -32602' because the server never issued one. If a page size is configurable, keep 0 = unlimited as default so all existing clients see one page. Make list order deterministic (sort by uri/name or preserve registration order) — TDictionary.Values order is unspecified, which also hurts client-side caching keyed on list content.
- Field availability by legacy revision (gate optional emission): `title` on Resource/ResourceTemplate/Prompt/PromptArgument and `_meta` on Resource/contents: 2025-06-18+; `icons`: 2025-11-25+; `annotations.lastModified`: 2025-06-18+; `resource_link` content in PromptMessage: 2025-06-18+ (2025-03-26 PromptMessage.content = text|image|audio|resource only); completion `context.arguments`: 2025-06-18+. Legacy JSON schemas leave additionalProperties open, so this gating is polish rather than a MUST.
- Base64 in Delphi: System.NetEncoding.TNetEncoding.Base64 inserts CR/LF every 76 characters; use `TBase64Encoding.Create(0)` (CharsPerLine = 0) or `TNetEncoding.Base64String` (Delphi 12) for `blob` / image `data` so the value is a single-line base64 string as the schema expects (@format byte).
- Modern `PaginatedRequest.params` is required (schema.ts 2026-07-28 line 1063: `params: PaginatedRequestParams`) because `_meta` is required, so `resources/list` without params is malformed -> -32602/400 in the modern era. This is enforced by the shared `_meta` validation (lifecycle area), not by the resources manager; the manager must still tolerate Params = nil for legacy requests.
- Subscriptions: modern `resources/updated` requires `subscriptions/listen` streams with `notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged` first and `_meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId` on every notification; legacy needs an HTTP GET SSE stream or stdio push. Since the server advertises listChanged:false / subscribe:false in both eras, nothing is required now; note that TMCPIdHTTPServer.HandleGetRequest opens and immediately closes an SSE stream (IdHTTPServer.pas:271-300), so even legacy list_changed delivery is not possible today — keep the flags false until a subscriptions manager exists.
- Streamable HTTP `Mcp-Name` header for resources/read is sourced from `params.uri`; resource URIs routinely contain characters outside the plain-ASCII token set (':' '/' are fine, but spaces/non-ASCII must be Base64-sentinel encoded), so the transport validation must decode `=?base64?...?=` before comparing with the body (XC-04).
- MRTR is shared across tools/call, prompts/get and resources/read; design the EMCPInputRequired exception + requestState HMAC helper once (Protocol layer) and reuse in ToolsManager, PromptsManager and ResourcesManager. Servers MUST NOT emit inputRequests for capabilities the client did not declare in `_meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities`, so the request context must expose them.
- Library-compat design rule for this whole area: never add members to IMCPResource, IMCPTool, IMCPCapabilityManager or IMCPManagerRegistry that would force third-party implementers to change (Delphi interfaces are not open for extension); use new GUID'd interfaces discovered with Supports(), new protected fields with safe defaults in TMCPResourceBase<T>/TMCPToolBase<T>, and additive class methods on TMCPRegistry. New units (MCPServer.Errors, MCPServer.Pagination, MCPServer.Prompt.Base, MCPServer.PromptsManager, MCPServer.CompletionManager) are opt-in; MCPServer.dpr, MCPServer.dproj (DCCReference list at /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/src/MCPServer.dproj:131-150) and build.bat search paths (`src\Prompts` must be added to the -U list at /home/user/Delphi-MCP-Server/build.bat) need updating.
- The repo has no test project (only LICENSE, README.md, build.bat, generate-ssl-cert.bat, settings.ini.example, src). Suggest a DUnitX project exercising TMCPJsonRpcProcessor end-to-end for: resultType/ttlMs/cacheScope present on modern list/read results and absent on legacy; -32602 vs -32002 by era with data.uri; -32603 on read exception; -32602 on missing uri and on any cursor; blob base64 round-trip; prompts/get -32602 on unknown prompt and missing required argument; prompts/get result has no ttlMs.
- TMCPServerStatusResource.SetNamePrefix re-registers a URI in TMCPRegistry at runtime (Resource.Server.pas:72-93); it only affects managers constructed afterwards, so RS-04 (no per-connection variation) is not violated, but document that registry changes after manager creation are not reflected (or add TMCPResourcesManager.Refresh + listChanged support later).
- Open question for the orchestrator: whether prompts, resource templates and completion are in scope for this release (all three are MAY features). If yes, the suggested designs above are additive and share the pagination/caching/error plumbing with tools, so building the plumbing first (GAP-RS-01/02, GAP-CA-01, GAP-PG-01) makes each optional feature roughly one unit of work.

### Requirements

- **RS-01** (MUST, both): "Servers that support resources MUST declare the `resources` capability" — modern: inside DiscoverResult.capabilities (server/discover); legacy: inside the `initialize` result. Shape: {"resources": {"subscribe"?: boolean, "listChanged"?: boolean}}; "Serves that support neither `listChanged` or `subscribe` may omit it" -> {"resources": {}}.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Capabilities; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2025-11-25/server/resources.mdx § Capabilities; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ServerCapabilities.resources_
- **RS-02** (MAY, modern): `subscribe` now means "whether the server supports resource-specific update notifications for resources requested through subscriptions/listen using the resourceSubscriptions filter"; `listChanged` = "whether the server will emit notifications when the list of available resources changes". "Servers may advertise either feature independently, together or neither."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Capabilities_
- **RS-03** (MAY, legacy): `subscribe`: "whether the client can subscribe to be notified of changes to individual resources" via `resources/subscribe` (params.uri) / `resources/unsubscribe`; server then sends `notifications/resources/updated` {uri}. "Both `subscribe` and `listChanged` are optional—servers can support neither, either, or both".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2025-11-25/server/resources.mdx § Capabilities, § Subscriptions; schema/2025-11-25/schema.ts SubscribeRequest, UnsubscribeRequest_
- **RS-04** (MUST, modern): "Servers that declare the `resources` capability MUST respond to `resources/list` requests with the set of resources currently available to the requesting client. This set MAY be empty and MAY change over time ..., but MUST NOT vary per-connection or as a side effect of other requests on the connection. The set MAY vary by the authorization presented on the request".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Capabilities_
- **RS-05** (MUST, both): `resources/list` result = {"resources": Resource[], "nextCursor"?: string} (legacy) and additionally REQUIRED {"resultType": "complete", "ttlMs": number>=0, "cacheScope": "public"|"private"} in modern (ListResourcesResult extends PaginatedResult, CacheableResult; JSON schema required = [cacheScope, resources, resultType, ttlMs]).  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ListResourcesResult; schema/2026-07-28/schema.json definitions.ListResourcesResult.required; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Listing Resources_
- **RS-06** (MUST, both): Resource object: `uri` (required, @format uri), `name` (required), `title`? (2025-06-18+), `description`?, `mimeType`? ("if known"), `size`? ("raw resource content, in bytes (i.e., before base64 encoding)"), `icons`? (2025-11-25+), `annotations`? {audience?, priority? 0..1, lastModified? ISO-8601 (2025-06-18+)}, `_meta`? (2025-06-18+). 2025-03-26 Resource has only uri,name,description,mimeType,size,annotations{audience,priority}.  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts Resource, Annotations, BaseMetadata, Icons; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Data Types › Resource, § Annotations; schema/2025-03-26/schema.json definitions.Resource_
- **RS-07** (MUST, both): `resources/read` request params: `uri` (required). Result: {"contents": (TextResourceContents|BlobResourceContents)[]} plus, modern only, REQUIRED `resultType`, `ttlMs`, `cacheScope` (ReadResourceResult extends CacheableResult). "Servers MAY return multiple resource contents in response to a single `resources/read` request."  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ResourceRequestParams, ReadResourceRequestParams, ReadResourceResult; schema/2026-07-28/schema.json definitions.ReadResourceResult.required=[cacheScope,contents,resultType,ttlMs]; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Reading Resources_
- **RS-08** (MUST, both): Text contents = {uri, mimeType?, text} ("text ... must only be set if the item can actually be represented as text (not binary data)"); Blob contents = {uri, mimeType?, blob: base64 string}. Security: "Binary data MUST be properly encoded".  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts TextResourceContents, BlobResourceContents; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Resource Contents, § Security Considerations (3)_
- **RS-09** (MAY, modern): "Servers MAY also respond to `resources/read` with an `InputRequiredResult` to indicate that additional input is needed before the resource can be read" (MRTR). Supported-request table lists prompts/get, resources/read, tools/call only; "Servers MUST NOT send `InputRequiredResult` responses on any other client requests." "Servers MUST include at least one of `inputRequests` or `requestState` in every `InputRequiredResult`"; "Servers MUST NOT send an `inputRequests` that the client has not declared support for in its capabilities"; requestState "MUST" be treated as attacker-controlled and integrity-protected if it influences authorization.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Reading Resources; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/mrtr.mdx § Supported Requests, § Server Requirements (Basic Workflow)_
- **RS-10** (MUST, modern): "If the requested resource does not exist, servers MUST return a JSON-RPC error with code `-32602` (Invalid Params). Servers SHOULD return `-32603` for internal errors." Example error carries "data": {"uri": ...}; SEP-2164: "The `data` field SHOULD include the `uri` that was not found."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Error Handling; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/seps/2164-resource-not-found-error.md § Specification_
- **RS-11** (MUST NOT, modern): "Servers MUST NOT return an empty `contents` array for a non-existent resource. An empty array is ambiguous".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Error Handling_
- **RS-12** (MUST NOT, modern): "Implementations of this protocol version MUST NOT emit these codes: `-32002` — resource not found (2025-11-25 and earlier; replaced by `-32602`)". Also "-32000 to -32019 — legacy ... new implementations SHOULD NOT use codes from this sub-range at all".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx § Error Codes_
- **RS-13** (SHOULD, legacy): "Servers SHOULD return standard JSON-RPC errors for common failure cases: Resource not found: `-32002`; Internal errors: `-32603`" (identical wording in 2025-03-26, 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25). Example: {"code": -32002, "message": "Resource not found", "data": {"uri": ...}}.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2025-11-25/server/resources.mdx § Error Handling; .../2025-06-18/server/resources.mdx § Error Handling (line 374); .../2025-03-26/server/resources.mdx § Error Handling (line 334)_
- **RS-14** (MUST, both): "1. Servers MUST validate all resource URIs"; "2. Access controls SHOULD be implemented for sensitive resources"; "4. Resource permissions SHOULD be checked before operations"; modern adds "5. Servers MUST sanitize file paths to prevent directory traversal attacks when serving `file://` resources". "Custom URI schemes MUST be in accordance with RFC3986".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Security Considerations, § Custom URI Schemes; 2025-11-25 same headings_
- **RS-15** (SHOULD, both): https://: "Servers SHOULD use this scheme only when the client is able to fetch and load the resource directly from the web on its own"; "For other use cases, servers SHOULD prefer to use another URI scheme, or define a custom one". file://: "MCP servers MAY identify file:// resources with an XDG MIME type, like `inode/directory`".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Common URI Schemes_
- **RS-16** (SHOULD, both): "When the list of available resources changes, servers that declared the `listChanged` capability SHOULD send a notification: notifications/resources/list_changed". Modern: only delivered on a subscriptions/listen stream where the client set `resourcesListChanged: true` ("the server MUST NOT send notification types the client has not explicitly requested"), tagged with `_meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId`. Legacy: sent unsolicited (HTTP GET SSE stream / stdio).  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § List Changed Notification; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ResourceListChangedNotification, SubscriptionFilter; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/subscriptions.mdx § Opening a Stream_
- **RS-17** (MUST, modern): `resources/subscribe` / `resources/unsubscribe` are removed (changelog #4: "Replace the HTTP GET endpoint and `resources/subscribe`/`resources/unsubscribe` with `subscriptions/listen`"). Subscriptions are opened via `subscriptions/listen` params.notifications.resourceSubscriptions: string[]; server "MUST send `notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged` as the first message" and delivers `notifications/resources/updated` {"_meta": {"io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId": <listen id>}, "uri"}. A modern client that still sends resources/subscribe hits "If the server does not implement the requested RPC method, it MUST respond with `404 Not Found` and a JSON-RPC error with code `-32601`" (HTTP).  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/changelog.mdx item 4; .../2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Subscriptions; .../2026-07-28/basic/patterns/subscriptions.mdx § Acknowledgment; .../2026-07-28/basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx § Protocol Version Header_
- **RS-18** (MUST, modern): Every result "MUST include a `resultType` field" ("complete" for ordinary results, "input_required" for MRTR interim results); applies to ListResourcesResult, ReadResourceResult, ListResourceTemplatesResult, ListPromptsResult, GetPromptResult, CompleteResult. "Servers implementing this protocol version MUST include this field."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx § Result Responses, § ResultType; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts Result.resultType_
- **RT-01** (MUST, both): `resources/templates/list` result = {"resourceTemplates": ResourceTemplate[], "nextCursor"?} (+ modern REQUIRED resultType, ttlMs, cacheScope). ResourceTemplate: `uriTemplate` (required, "A URI template (according to RFC 6570)"), `name` (required), `title`?, `description`?, `mimeType`? ("should only be included if all resources matching this template have the same type"), `icons`?, `annotations`?, `_meta`?.  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ResourceTemplate, ListResourceTemplatesResult; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Resource Templates_
- **RT-02** (MAY, both): "Resource templates allow servers to expose parameterized resources using URI templates. Arguments may be auto-completed through the completion API." (ref/resource with uri = the template). Supports pagination and (modern) caching.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/resources.mdx § Resource Templates; .../2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion.mdx § Reference Types_
- **PR-01** (MUST, both): "Servers that support prompts MUST declare the `prompts` capability" ({"prompts": {"listChanged"?: boolean}}) — modern in DiscoverResult, legacy "during initialization". Minimum baseline example: {"prompts": {}}.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Capabilities; .../2025-11-25/server/prompts.mdx § Capabilities; schema/2026-07-28/examples/ServerCapabilities/prompts-minimum-baseline-support.json_
- **PR-02** (MUST, modern): "Servers that declare the `prompts` capability MUST respond to `prompts/list` requests with the set of prompts currently available to the requesting client. This set MAY be empty and MAY change over time ..., but MUST NOT vary per-connection or as a side effect of other requests on the connection."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Capabilities_
- **PR-03** (MUST, both): `prompts/list` result = {"prompts": Prompt[], "nextCursor"?} (+ modern REQUIRED resultType, ttlMs, cacheScope; ListPromptsResult extends PaginatedResult, CacheableResult). Prompt: `name` (required), `title`? (2025-06-18+), `description`?, `arguments`?: PromptArgument[] {name (required), title?, description?, required?: boolean}, `icons`? (2025-11-25+), `_meta`?.  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ListPromptsResult, Prompt, PromptArgument; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Listing Prompts, § Data Types › Prompt_
- **PR-04** (MUST, both): `prompts/get` params: `name` (required), `arguments`?: {[key]: string}. Result: {"description"?: string, "messages": PromptMessage[]} where PromptMessage = {"role": "user"|"assistant", "content": ContentBlock}; modern adds REQUIRED resultType. GetPromptResult is NOT a CacheableResult (no ttlMs/cacheScope).  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts GetPromptRequestParams, GetPromptResult, PromptMessage, Role; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Getting a Prompt_
- **PR-05** (MUST, both): Prompt message content types: text {type:'text', text}; image {type:'image', data (base64), mimeType} — "The image data MUST be base64-encoded and include a valid MIME type"; audio {type:'audio', data, mimeType} — "MUST be base64-encoded and include a valid MIME type"; resource_link {type:'resource_link', uri, name, ...Resource fields} (2025-06-18+ only; absent from 2025-03-26 PromptMessage.content); embedded resource {type:'resource', resource: TextResourceContents|BlobResourceContents} — "MUST include: A valid resource URI; The appropriate MIME type; Either text content or base64-encoded blob data". "All content types in prompt messages support optional annotations".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § PromptMessage (Text/Image/Audio/Resource Links/Embedded Resources); schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts ContentBlock, EmbeddedResource, ResourceLink; schema/2025-03-26/schema.ts PromptMessage (line 643)_
- **PR-06** (MAY, modern): "Servers MAY also respond to `prompts/get` with an `InputRequiredResult` to indicate that additional input is needed before the prompt can be resolved." (GetPromptResultResponse.result: GetPromptResult | InputRequiredResult). Same MRTR server requirements as RS-09.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Getting a Prompt; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts GetPromptResultResponse; .../basic/patterns/mrtr.mdx_
- **PR-07** (SHOULD, both): "Servers SHOULD return standard JSON-RPC errors for common failure cases: Invalid prompt name: `-32602` (Invalid params); Missing required arguments: `-32602` (Invalid params); Internal errors: `-32603` (Internal error)".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Error Handling; 2025-11-25 same heading_
- **PR-08** (MUST, both): "Servers SHOULD validate prompt arguments before processing"; "Both parties SHOULD respect capability negotiation"; "Implementations MUST carefully validate all prompt inputs and outputs to prevent injection attacks or unauthorized access to resources."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § Implementation Considerations, § Security_
- **PR-09** (SHOULD, both): "When the list of available prompts changes, servers that declared the `listChanged` capability SHOULD send a notification" `notifications/prompts/list_changed` — modern: only "to clients that have opened a `subscriptions/listen` stream with `promptsListChanged: true`".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/prompts.mdx § List Changed Notification_
- **CA-01** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST include caching hints on results with `resultType: "complete"` returned by the following operations: `server/discover`, `tools/list`, `prompts/list`, `resources/list`, `resources/templates/list`, `resources/read`". "Interim results with `resultType: "input_required"` ... are not cacheable and carry no caching hints."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Cacheable Results; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts CacheableResult_
- **CA-02** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST provide a `ttlMs` value that is `>= 0`" (integer milliseconds; 0 = immediately stale; absent "should only occur in older server versions"; negative ignored by clients).  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Time-to-Live (TTL) Field; schema.ts CacheableResult.ttlMs @minimum 0_
- **CA-03** (MUST, modern): `cacheScope` is required, value "public" ("does not contain user-specific data ... MAY store and serve ... to any user") or "private" ("Caches MUST NOT be shared across authorization contexts"). Guidance: "`"public"` is appropriate for lists of tools, prompts, and resource templates when they are identical for all users. `"private"` is appropriate for `resources/read` results that depend on the authenticated user, or for filtered list results that vary per user." SEP-2549: "`cacheScope` is required because there is no safe default for older servers."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Cache Scope Field, § Choosing a Cache Scope; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/seps/2549-TTL-for-list-results.md § Backward Compatibility_
- **CA-04** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST apply the same `cacheScope` to all response pages for a given list request." Each page carries its own ttlMs; "Servers MAY return different `ttlMs` values on different pages".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Interaction with Pagination_
- **CA-05** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST be aware that responses with a `"public"` `cacheScope` may be shared between callers even if the Result is coming from an authenticated endpoint." Server implementors "MUST apply appropriate per-primitive access controls, and MUST NOT rely on `cacheScope` alone to prevent unauthorized access".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Security Considerations_
- **CA-06** (MAY, modern): "A server MAY provide `ttlMs` without advertising `listChanged: true`"; "TTL is a freshness hint, not a guarantee. Servers MAY change the underlying data before the TTL expires." Results of MRTR retries (requests carrying inputResponses/requestState) "MUST NOT be cached" by clients — server still must include fields on complete results.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/caching.mdx § Interaction with Notifications, § Cache Key, § TTL note_
- **PG-01** (MAY, both): Pagination "uses an opaque cursor-based approach": "The cursor is an opaque string token"; "Page size is determined by the server". Response: "The current page of results; An optional `nextCursor` field if more results exist". Request continues with params.cursor. Operations: `resources/list`, `resources/templates/list`, `prompts/list`, `tools/list`.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination.mdx § Pagination Model, § Response Format, § Request Format, § Operations Supporting Pagination; schema.ts PaginatedRequestParams, PaginatedResult_
- **PG-02** (SHOULD, both): "Servers SHOULD: Provide stable cursors; Handle invalid cursors gracefully".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination.mdx § Implementation Guidelines_
- **PG-03** (SHOULD, both): "Invalid cursors SHOULD result in an error with code -32602 (Invalid params)."  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination.mdx § Error Handling; 2025-11-25 same heading_
- **PG-04** (MUST NOT, modern): Clients "MUST NOT" treat an empty-string cursor as end of results ("an empty string is a valid cursor") — therefore a server signals end-of-list only by OMITTING `nextCursor`, never by emitting "".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/pagination.mdx § Implementation Guidelines (3)_
- **PG-05** (SHOULD, modern): "Servers SHOULD return tools from `tools/list` in a deterministic order to enable client-side caching" (changelog); by extension stable cursors (PG-02) require a deterministic order for resources/prompts lists too.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/changelog.mdx item 3 (Minor changes)_
- **CO-01** (MUST, both): "Servers that support completions MUST declare the `completions` capability": {"completions": {}}.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion.mdx § Capabilities; 2025-11-25 same_
- **CO-02** (MUST, both): `completion/complete` params: `ref` = {type:'ref/prompt', name} | {type:'ref/resource', uri (URI or URI template)}; `argument` {name, value}; `context`?.{arguments?: {[k]: string}} (context added 2025-06-18; absent in 2025-03-26). Result: {"completion": {"values": string[] ("Must not exceed 100 items"), "total"?: number, "hasMore"?: boolean}} (+ modern resultType; CompleteResult is not cacheable).  
  _Source: schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts CompleteRequestParams, CompleteResult, PromptReference, ResourceTemplateReference; /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion.mdx § Data Types, § Completion Results; schema/2025-03-26/schema.ts CompleteRequest (line 1095, no context)_
- **CO-03** (SHOULD, both): "Servers SHOULD return standard JSON-RPC errors for common failure cases: Method not found: `-32601` (Capability not supported); Invalid prompt name: `-32602`; Missing required arguments: `-32602`; Internal errors: `-32603`".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion.mdx § Error Handling_
- **CO-04** (MUST, both): "Servers SHOULD: Return suggestions sorted by relevance; Implement fuzzy matching where appropriate; Rate limit completion requests; Validate all inputs". "Implementations MUST: Validate all completion inputs; Implement appropriate rate limiting; Control access to sensitive suggestions; Prevent completion-based information disclosure".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/server/utilities/completion.mdx § Implementation Considerations, § Security_
- **XC-01** (MUST, modern): HTTP: "If the server does not implement the requested RPC method, it MUST respond with `404 Not Found` and a JSON-RPC error with code `-32601`" — applies to `resources/subscribe`, `resources/unsubscribe`, and to `prompts/*` / `completion/complete` when those capabilities are not offered.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx § Protocol Version Header_
- **XC-02** (SHOULD, modern): "Servers SHOULD include the following `io.modelcontextprotocol/*` field in every result's `_meta` ... `io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo`" (Implementation {name, version}).  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx § `_meta` › Per-response protocol fields; schema.ts ResultMetaObject_
- **XC-03** (MUST, modern): Every request "MUST include the required `_meta` fields" (protocolVersion, clientCapabilities); "A request missing any required field is malformed; the server MUST reject it with JSON-RPC error code `-32602` (Invalid params). On HTTP, the response status MUST be `400 Bad Request`." Note PaginatedRequest.params is non-optional in 2026-07-28 (`params: PaginatedRequestParams`), so a modern `resources/list` with no params object is malformed.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/index.mdx § `_meta` › Per-request protocol fields; schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts PaginatedRequest (line 1063) vs schema/2025-11-25/schema.ts PaginatedRequest (params?)_
- **XC-04** (MUST, modern): Streamable HTTP standard headers: `Mcp-Method` (all requests) and `Mcp-Name` sourced from "`params.name` or `params.uri`" for "`tools/call`, `resources/read`, `prompts/get` requests" are REQUIRED; "Servers that process the request body MUST reject requests where the values specified in the headers do not match the corresponding values in the request body" with `400 Bad Request` and `-32020` (HeaderMismatch); values may be Base64-sentinel encoded (`=?base64?...?=`) and "servers MUST decode encoded values".  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx § Standard Request Headers, § Value Encoding, § Server Validation_
- **XC-05** (MAY, both): Dual-era server rule: "A request carrying modern per-request `_meta` is served statelessly according to this revision. An `initialize` request selects legacy semantics, scoped to the stdio process (stdio) or the session (HTTP), as specified by the negotiated legacy protocol version." "A dual-era server MAY serve both eras concurrently on the same endpoint or process." Consequence for this area: error code (-32602 vs -32002), resultType/ttlMs/cacheScope emission and subscribe-method availability must be selected per request from the negotiated revision.  
  _Source: /home/user/modelcontextprotocol/modelcontextprotocol/docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/versioning.mdx § Backward Compatibility with Initialization-Based Versions (after Compatibility Matrix)_

## MRTR, subscriptions/listen, elicitation, deprecated features (roots/sampling/logging), extensions field, tasks extension — server-side requirements for a dual-era Delphi MCP server

Requirements extracted: 48. Gaps: 17.


### GAP-CTX-01 [major, effort M] No per-request context (era, _meta, clientCapabilities, transport) reaches managers/tools — root enabler for every MUST in this area

- Requirements: MRTR-08, MRTR-15, LG-03, LG-04, SUB-01, SUB-02, EL-02, TSK-02
- Current: TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest(RequestBody, SessionID) (src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:146-224) extracts only id/method/params and calls IMCPCapabilityManager.ExecuteMethod(Method, Params) (src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:265-270, JsonRpcProcessor.pas:113-126). params._meta is never read; nothing knows whether the caller is a legacy session (initialize, CoreManager.pas:70-140) or a modern request; IMCPTool.Execute(Arguments) (src/Tools/MCPServer.Tool.Base.pas:18) and IMCPResource.Read (src/Resources/MCPServer.Resource.Base.pas:247) receive no context either. Tool instances are singletons shared across Indy worker threads (ToolsManager.pas:245-248), so context cannot be stored on the tool.
- Required: Servers MUST NOT rely on undeclared client capabilities and MUST NOT infer them from prior requests (MRTR-08); MUST NOT emit notifications/message without io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel (LG-03); MUST honor only the requested notification filter (SUB-01); MUST only use negotiated capabilities in legacy sessions (MRTR-15).
- Compat impact: Library consumers: keep IMCPCapabilityManager, IMCPTool, IMCPResource GUIDs and signatures unchanged; add opt-in extension interfaces so existing custom managers/tools/resources compile and behave identically (they simply never see the context). Legacy clients: no wire change.
- Suggested change: New unit Protocol/MCPServer.RequestContext.pas: IMCPRequestContext = interface with ProtocolVersion: string; Era: TMCPProtocolEra (peLegacy, peModern); ClientCapabilities: TJSONObject (read-only, may be nil for legacy pre-initialize); ClientInfo: TJSONObject; LogLevel: string; RequestID: TValue; InputResponses: TJSONObject; RequestState: string; Transport hooks (IMCPNotificationSink for streams). TMCPJsonRpcProcessor builds it from params._meta (modern) or from the CoreManager's negotiated legacy session (legacy). Add IMCPCapabilityManagerV2 = interface(IMCPCapabilityManager) ['new GUID'] function ExecuteMethodEx(const Method: string; const Params: TJSONObject; const Context: IMCPRequestContext): TValue; processor does `if Supports(Manager, IMCPCapabilityManagerV2, V2) then V2.ExecuteMethodEx(...) else Manager.ExecuteMethod(...)`. Same pattern for tools: IMCPContextAwareTool ['GUID'] function ExecuteWithContext(const Arguments: TJSONObject; const Context: IMCPRequestContext): TValue; TMCPToolBase/TMCPToolBase<T>/<T,R> implement it by default as Execute(Arguments) and expose a protected virtual ExecuteWithParams overload taking Context. Never store context in a field of the singleton tool; pass it as a parameter (or a threadvar as last resort).

### GAP-ERR-01 [major, effort S] Error plumbing cannot express -32021 (MissingRequiredClientCapability) with data.requiredCapabilities, nor -32602 for invalid input

- Requirements: MRTR-08, MRTR-10, LG-04, TSK-02, SUB-09
- Current: CreateErrorResponse(RequestID, ErrorCode, ErrorMessage) (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:128-144) has no `data` member; error code selection is by substring: Pos('not found', E.Message) → -32601, else -32603 (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:211-217); only five constants exist (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:27-32, duplicated in IdHTTPServer.pas:94-99). No -32602 is ever emitted; HTTP status is always 200 (IdHTTPServer.pas:509,553).
- Required: MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError -32021 with data.requiredCapabilities (basic/index.mdx _meta; schema MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError), HTTP 400; invalid logLevel / malformed inputResponses → -32602; error responses MAY include data.
- Compat impact: Additive; existing consumers raising plain Exception keep getting -32603/-32601 as today. Legacy clients unaffected. (HTTP status mapping itself belongs to the transport area.)
- Suggested change: In MCPServer.Types.pas add consts MCP_ERROR_HEADER_MISMATCH = -32020; MCP_ERROR_MISSING_CLIENT_CAPABILITY = -32021; MCP_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSION = -32022; and class EMCPJsonRpcError = class(Exception) with Code: Integer and Data: TJSONObject (owned), plus helpers EMCPMissingClientCapability.Create(const RequiredCapabilities: TJSONObject) and EMCPInvalidParams.Create(const Msg). TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest catches `on E: EMCPJsonRpcError` first and emits code + data verbatim; CreateErrorResponse gains an optional `Data: TJSONObject` parameter. Expose the resulting code to the transport (e.g. ProcessRequest returns a record {Body: string; ErrorCode: Integer}) so IdHTTPServer can map -32021/-32022/-32020 → 400 and -32601 → 404 for modern requests.

### GAP-MRTR-02 [major, effort S] No enforcement that inputRequests only use capabilities the client declared on THIS request (elicitation form/url, sampling(.tools), roots) and no -32021 path

- Requirements: MRTR-08, EL-02, RS-02
- Current: Client capabilities are never parsed: CoreManager.Initialize only logs clientInfo (CoreManager.pas:83-95) and discards params.capabilities; modern _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities is never read (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:185-190 only passes params through).
- Required: "Servers MUST NOT send an inputRequests that the client has not declared support for"; elicitation {} means form only; "Servers MUST NOT send elicitation requests with modes that are not supported by the client"; "Servers MUST NOT send tool-enabled sampling requests to Clients that have not declared ... sampling.tools"; when the request cannot proceed without the capability → -32021 with data.requiredCapabilities (HTTP 400).
- Compat impact: Only affects consumers who opt into MRTR. Legacy sessions: capability snapshot comes from initialize params and is stored in the session/process (allowed for legacy semantics); modern: from each request's _meta and never cached.
- Suggested change: In TMCPToolsManager (and ResourcesManager) before serializing an EMCPInputRequired: for each inputRequests value check Context.ClientCapabilities — 'elicitation/create' requires capabilities.elicitation present; mode 'url' additionally requires elicitation.url; mode 'form'/absent requires elicitation.form or an empty elicitation object; 'sampling/createMessage' requires sampling (and sampling.tools when params.tools present); 'roots/list' requires roots. On failure raise EMCPMissingClientCapability with data {"requiredCapabilities": {"elicitation": {"url": {}}}} (shape per schema/2026-07-28/examples/MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError/missing-elicitation-capability.json). Store the legacy initialize `capabilities` object in TMCPCoreManager (new property ClientCapabilities: TJSONObject, cloned) so the same check runs for legacy sessions if legacy elicitation is ever added; for modern requests read _meta per request only.

### GAP-MRTR-03 [major, effort M] No integrity-protected requestState helper (HMAC/AEAD, TTL, principal, request digest)

- Requirements: MRTR-05, MRTR-06, EL-06
- Current: Nothing in src/ signs, verifies or even reads params.requestState; ExtractToolNameAndArguments (ToolsManager.pas:252-274) ignores every param except name/arguments. TMCPSettings (src/Core/MCPServer.Settings.pas) has no secret-key setting.
- Required: "servers MUST treat requestState as an attacker-controlled input ... MUST protect its integrity (e.g. HMAC or AEAD) and MUST reject state that fails verification"; SHOULD embed and verify principal, TTL, and originating-request identifier (method + digest of salient params).
- Compat impact: Additive helper; no effect on consumers who do not use MRTR. Operators running multiple instances behind a load balancer must configure a shared key (new settings.ini [Security] RequestStateKey); a per-process random key is generated (and logged as a warning) when absent.
- Suggested change: New unit Core/MCPServer.RequestState.pas: TMCPRequestState with class function Seal(const Payload: TJSONObject; const Context: IMCPRequestContext; const Method: string; const ArgsDigest: string; TTLSeconds: Integer): string — serializes {p: payload, m: method, d: ArgsDigest, u: principal-or-'', exp: unix} as base64url (System.NetEncoding TBase64URLEncoding) and appends '.' + base64url(HMAC-SHA256) via System.Hash.THashSHA2.GetHMACAsBytes(Data, Key, SHA256); class function Open(const Token: string; const Context; const Method; const ArgsDigest; out Payload: TJSONObject): Boolean using constant-time compare, checking exp, m, d and u. ArgsDigest = SHA-256 of canonical JSON of params.arguments (or uri). On verification failure: raise EMCPInvalidParams('Invalid request state') (-32602) or, per MRTR-12, return a fresh InputRequiredResult without trusting the state. Key source: TMCPSettings.RequestStateKey (base64), else random 32 bytes at startup. Document that single-use semantics (one-time redemption) are the tool author's responsibility.

### GAP-MRTR-04 [major, effort S] No era gating: an InputRequiredResult (and resultType) must never be sent to a legacy-era client; resultType must be added for modern-era results

- Requirements: MRTR-15, MRTR-13
- Current: All results are emitted verbatim from managers (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:192-200); there is no notion of era, and no resultType is ever added. MCP_PROTOCOL_VERSION is the single constant '2025-06-18' (Types.pas:237) echoed by Initialize (CoreManager.pas:101).
- Required: Legacy: "Only use capabilities that were successfully negotiated" — legacy clients do not understand resultType/input_required (they would treat the object as a CallToolResult lacking `content`). Modern: "The result MUST include a resultType field"; "Servers implementing this protocol version MUST include this field."
- Compat impact: Legacy clients (Claude Code, Codex, Claude Desktop, VS Code, Inspector default) keep receiving byte-identical results if resultType is only injected for modern-era requests. Library consumers who raise EMCPInputRequired get a defined legacy fallback instead of a broken response.
- Suggested change: In TMCPJsonRpcProcessor.ProcessRequest: when Context.Era = peModern and the result is a TJSONObject without 'resultType', AddPair('resultType','complete') (InputRequiredResult already carries 'input_required'). In TMCPToolsManager/ResourcesManager: when an EMCPInputRequired escapes under Context.Era = peLegacy, convert it — tools: CallToolResult {content:[{type:text,text:'This tool needs additional input (elicitation: <message>) which requires MCP 2026-07-28; please supply the information as tool arguments'}], isError:true}; resources: -32603 internal error or a text content explaining the same. Optionally (not recommended, see notes) implement legacy in-stream elicitation/create over SSE later. Add a unit test matrix: modern tools/call → input_required; legacy tools/call → isError text.

### GAP-MRTR-05 [minor, effort S] Structural guarantee that InputRequiredResult is only produced for tools/call, resources/read, prompts/get

- Requirements: MRTR-03
- Current: Once EMCPInputRequired exists, any manager method (e.g. a custom IMCPCapabilityManager implementing tools/list) could leak it; today the generic handler turns unknown exceptions into -32603 (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:204-218), which is accidentally correct.
- Required: "Servers MUST NOT send InputRequiredResult responses on any other client requests."
- Compat impact: None for consumers; defensive.
- Suggested change: Catch EMCPInputRequired only inside TMCPToolsManager.CallTool, TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource and TMCPPromptsManager.GetPrompt; in TMCPJsonRpcProcessor treat any EMCPInputRequired that reaches it as an internal error (-32603, message 'InputRequiredResult not permitted for method X') and log it; document in README that custom managers must not return resultType input_required except from those three methods.

### GAP-MRTR-06 [minor, effort M] inputResponses parsing/validation semantics (ignore unknown keys, re-request missing keys, handle decline/cancel, validate against requestedSchema)

- Requirements: MRTR-10, MRTR-11, MRTR-12, EL-05, EL-08
- Current: params.inputResponses is silently dropped (ToolsManager.pas:264-273 reads only name/arguments) — consistent with 'ignore unrecognized' but provides no parsed access, no ElicitResult model, no schema validation.
- Required: SHOULD validate InputResponses; SHOULD ignore unknown entries; SHOULD answer missing-but-necessary info with a new InputRequiredResult rather than an error; MUST handle decline/cancel; SHOULD validate content against requestedSchema.
- Compat impact: Additive helper API; no wire impact.
- Suggested change: In Protocol/MCPServer.Mrtr.pas: TMCPInputResponses wrapper over the TJSONObject with TryGetElicit(key, out R: TMCPElicitResult): Boolean (Action: accept|decline|cancel, Content: TJSONObject), TryGetSampling(key, out TJSONObject), TryGetRoots(key, out TJSONArray); malformed value (not an object / bad action) → raise EMCPInvalidParams (-32602); unknown keys ignored. Provide TMCPElicitSchema.Validate(Content, RequestedSchema): TArray<string> covering type, required, enum/oneOf/anyOf-const, minLength/maxLength, minimum/maximum, minItems/maxItems, format email/uri/date/date-time. Pattern documented in README: if TryGetElicit fails or Action <> accept, either finish with an isError result or raise a new EMCPInputRequired (MRTR-12).

### GAP-LG-01 [minor, effort S] io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel in modern _meta is not validated (SHOULD reject unknown values with -32602)

- Requirements: LG-04
- Current: _meta is never inspected (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:185-190). No notifications/message are emitted anywhere, so LG-03 is trivially met, but an invalid level value is accepted silently.
- Required: "If the io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel value carried in a request's _meta is not a recognized log level, the server SHOULD reject that request with ... -32602 (Invalid params)". The MCP Inspector stamps logLevel=debug on every modern request by default (docs/docs/2026-07-28/tools/inspector/protocol-eras.mdx, Logging/Modern), so the key WILL appear in practice.
- Compat impact: None for legacy; modern clients sending a bogus level get -32602 instead of silent acceptance.
- Suggested change: In the _meta parser (GAP-CTX-01): if 'io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel' is present and not in ['debug','info','notice','warning','error','critical','alert','emergency'] raise EMCPInvalidParams('Invalid log level'). Store the level on IMCPRequestContext.LogLevel for information only. Do NOT add a `logging` capability, `logging/setLevel` handling, or `notifications/message` emission (deprecated, LG-01); keep TLogger → stderr/file. Optionally log a TLogger.Debug once per process that a client requested protocol logging which this server does not provide.

### GAP-LG-02 [minor, effort S] No warning when deprecated capabilities are negotiated (roots/sampling in client capabilities, or a legacy logging/setLevel call)

- Requirements: RS-03
- Current: CoreManager.Initialize ignores params.capabilities (CoreManager.pas:83-95); logging/setLevel from a legacy client yields -32601 'Method [logging/setLevel] not found' (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:121-123, 214-215) with no diagnostic.
- Required: SEP-2577: "Implementations SHOULD emit a warning (e.g., in logs or developer tooling) when deprecated capabilities are negotiated."
- Compat impact: Log-only; no wire change.
- Suggested change: In TMCPCoreManager.Initialize (legacy) and in the modern _meta parser: if clientCapabilities contains 'roots' or 'sampling', TLogger.Warning('Client declares deprecated capability %s (SEP-2577); this server does not use it'). Keep -32601 for logging/setLevel and resources/subscribe (capability not advertised) but log at Info level that the method is unsupported/deprecated.

### GAP-SUB-02 [minor, effort S] listChanged/subscribe stay false — correct today, but must remain in sync with any future dynamic registration and must be reported through server/discover for modern clients

- Requirements: SUB-08, SUB-10
- Current: CoreManager advertises resources {subscribe:false, listChanged:false} and tools {supportsProgress:false, supportsCancellation:false} (non-standard keys; CoreManager.pas:106-124). Tool/resource sets are built once from TMCPRegistry at manager construction (ToolsManager.pas:240-250; ResourcesManager.pas:497-505), so the advertised 'false' is truthful and no list_changed notification is ever owed.
- Required: "servers that declared the listChanged capability SHOULD send a notification" (legacy: at any time; modern: on listen streams with the matching filter). Servers may advertise neither feature.
- Compat impact: Keep false unless GAP-SUB-01 Phase B ships. Removing the non-standard supportsProgress/supportsCancellation keys is harmless for legacy clients (unknown keys are ignored by all SDKs) but is another area's concern.
- Suggested change: Centralize capability construction in one function (e.g. TMCPCapabilityBuilder.Build(Era): TJSONObject) used by both TMCPCoreManager.Initialize (legacy) and the new server/discover handler (modern) so that tools.listChanged / resources.subscribe / resources.listChanged / prompts.listChanged and (modern only) extensions are declared from a single source of truth; add TMCPRegistry.OnChanged event so that, if runtime registration is added later, the SubscriptionsManager can fan out list_changed and the builder can flip listChanged to true.

### GAP-SUB-03 [minor, effort S] Legacy HTTP GET SSE stream returns 200 text/event-stream and immediately closes; modern GET should be 405

- Requirements: SUB-10
- Current: HandleGetRequest (IdHTTPServer.pas:271-314): with Accept: text/event-stream it answers 200, Content-Type text/event-stream, empty body, closes; otherwise a JSON info blob. The comment at :296-298 acknowledges 'closes stream immediately'.
- Required: Legacy: server "MUST either return Content-Type: text/event-stream in response to this HTTP GET, or else return HTTP 405 Method Not Allowed, indicating that the server does not offer an SSE stream". Modern-only: "HTTP GET or DELETE to the MCP endpoint: respond with 405" (SHOULD). GET is removed in 2026-07-28 (replaced by subscriptions/listen).
- Compat impact: Returning 405 is explicitly allowed by legacy clients (Claude Code/Inspector treat 405 as 'no standalone stream' and continue); an immediately-closed 200 stream can trigger reconnect loops in some clients. Overlaps the transport area.
- Suggested change: HandleGetRequest: return 405 Method Not Allowed (with Allow: POST, OPTIONS) for every GET to the MCP endpoint regardless of Accept, unless GAP-SUB-01 later implements a legacy standalone stream. Keep the non-SSE JSON info response only if the project wants it for browsers, but on a different path.

### GAP-MRTR-01 [optional, effort L] No way for a tool or resource to return an InputRequiredResult (MRTR) — elicitation is impossible in the modern era

- Requirements: MRTR-02, MRTR-04, MRTR-07, MRTR-09, MRTR-12, MRTR-14, EL-04
- Current: TMCPToolsManager.CallTool (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:386-407) always wraps the TValue into a CallToolResult via BuildToolCallResponse (286-339): string → content/isError, TJSONObject → structuredContent, TJSONArray → content. TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource (src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:538-591) always returns contents[]. IMCPTool.Execute returns TValue (Tool.Base.pas:18), TMCPToolBase<T>.ExecuteWithParams returns string (Tool.Base.pas:49), IMCPResource.Read returns string (Resource.Base.pas:247). No type for inputRequests/requestState exists anywhere in src/. There is no prompts manager at all.
- Required: Servers MAY answer tools/call, resources/read, prompts/get with {"resultType":"input_required","inputRequests":{key:{method,params}},"requestState":string} containing at least one of the two fields, keys unique per request, values restricted to elicitation/create | sampling/createMessage | roots/list; MAY return it repeatedly; the retry arrives as a NEW request carrying inputResponses/requestState.
- Compat impact: Purely additive for library consumers: existing TMCPToolBase<T>/TMCPToolBase<T,R>/TMCPResourceBase<T> subclasses keep compiling; MRTR is opt-in via a new exception type or a new return type. Legacy clients never see it (see GAP-MRTR-04). This is the only sanctioned path to elicitation for modern clients, so although MAY, it is the headline feature of the upgrade.
- Suggested change: New unit Protocol/MCPServer.Mrtr.pas: TMCPInputRequiredResult = class (InputRequests: TJSONObject map; RequestState: string; function ToJSON: TJSONObject builds resultType input_required and raises if both are empty — MRTR-07); EMCPInputRequired = class(Exception) carrying an owned TMCPInputRequiredResult so any tool/resource/prompt code can `raise EMCPInputRequired.Create(Result)`. TMCPToolsManager.CallTool and TMCPResourcesManager.ReadResource (and the future PromptsManager.GetPrompt) catch EMCPInputRequired (only in ExecuteTool/Read — not in list methods) and return its JSON as the result. Provide builders: TMCPInputRequests.AddElicitationForm(key, message, schema: TJSONObject), AddElicitationUrl(key, message, url), AddSampling(key, params), AddRoots(key); enforce key uniqueness (MRTR-04) via TDictionary. Extend TMCPToolBase<T> with protected `InputResponses: TJSONObject` / `RequestState: string` accessors taken from the IMCPRequestContext passed by the manager (GAP-CTX-01), plus helper `function ElicitResultFor(const Key: string): TMCPElicitResult` (see GAP-EL-01). Keep the SSE path unchanged: HandlePostRequestSSE (IdHTTPServer.pas:470-510) already emits exactly one event, satisfying MRTR-14.

### GAP-EL-01 [optional, effort M] No elicitation API: form/URL request builders, mode gating, restricted schema builder, URL-mode safety rules

- Requirements: EL-01, EL-02, EL-03, EL-07, EL-09
- Current: No elicitation code exists in src/. (The absence of -32042/elicitationId/notifications/elicitation/complete is conformant for 2026-07-28.)
- Required: Elicitation requests MUST carry message (+ requestedSchema for form; + url for url mode; mode 'url' explicit); schema restricted to flat primitives; MUST NOT use form mode for secrets; MUST NOT put sensitive data or pre-authenticated links in URLs; MUST NOT send unsupported modes; MUST NOT emit -32042 in modern era.
- Compat impact: Additive; library consumers get TMCPElicitation helpers; nothing existing changes.
- Suggested change: New unit Protocol/MCPServer.Elicitation.pas: TMCPElicitSchemaBuilder (AddString(name, title, description, required, minLength, maxLength, format, default), AddNumber/AddInteger(min,max,default), AddBoolean(default), AddEnum(values, titles?), AddMultiSelect(values, minItems, maxItems)) producing {type:object, properties, required}; TMCPElicitation.Form(message, schema) → {method:'elicitation/create', params:{mode:'form', message, requestedSchema}} and TMCPElicitation.Url(message, url) → {mode:'url', message, url} with a guard that url is absolute and, unless settings allow, https; TMCPElicitResult parser (accept/decline/cancel). Mode gating is done in the manager (GAP-MRTR-02). Add a README section 'Elicitation (MCP 2026-07-28)' with the dual-era caveat and the security rules (no secrets via form; bind URL flows to the authenticated user; never pre-authenticate URLs).

### GAP-SUB-01 [optional, effort L] subscriptions/listen not implemented and no long-lived stream infrastructure (HTTP SSE stays open; stdio interleaving)

- Requirements: SUB-01, SUB-02, SUB-03, SUB-04, SUB-05, SUB-06, SUB-07, SUB-09
- Current: subscriptions/listen falls through GetManagerForMethod → nil (ManagerRegistry.pas:171-184) → '-32601 Method [subscriptions/listen] not found' with HTTP 200 (IdHTTPServer.pas:553). HandlePostRequestSSE writes one event into ResponseInfo.ContentText and returns (IdHTTPServer.pas:470-510) — Indy sends the whole response after the handler returns, so no stream can stay open and nothing can be pushed later. Stdio loop is strictly synchronous Readln→ProcessRequest→Writeln (StdioTransport.pas:611-653) with no output lock and no way to write asynchronous notifications. X-Accel-Buffering: no is already set (IdHTTPServer.pas:287,483).
- Required: If the server offers the method: MUST send notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged first with _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId = listen request id and only the agreed filter subset; MUST tag every stream notification with subscriptionId; MUST NOT send unrequested types; SHOULD send completion result {resultType:'complete',_meta:{subscriptionId}} on server-initiated teardown; MUST send notifications/cancelled referencing the listen id when tearing down; HTTP client disconnect = cancellation (MUST NOT write further); stdio notifications/cancelled → stop; keep-alive SSE comments encouraged. If NOT offered: -32601 (and HTTP 404 in modern era — transport area).
- Compat impact: Not offering listen is compliant because CoreManager advertises resources.subscribe=false/listChanged=false and tools has no listChanged (CoreManager.pas:106-124) and lists are static. But the MCP Inspector's modern-era 'Subscribe' button sends subscriptions/listen and expects an acknowledgment (protocol-eras.mdx, Resource subscriptions/Modern), and modern hosts may open a toolsListChanged stream; an -32601 there is legal but unfriendly. Library consumers: additive (new optional TMCPSubscriptionsManager + IMCPNotificationSink); TMCPIdHTTPServer/TMCPStdioTransport public surface unchanged. Each open HTTP listen stream occupies one Indy worker thread (mind MaxConnections).
- Suggested change: Phase A (recommended minimum): new Managers/MCPServer.SubscriptionsManager.pas implementing IMCPCapabilityManagerV2 for 'subscriptions/listen' (modern era only; legacy → -32601): validate params.notifications; compute agreed subset (currently {} because nothing is advertised — but include resourceSubscriptions entries only for URIs that exist AND only if resources.subscribe is advertised); register a TMCPSubscription {Id: RequestId; Filter; Sink: IMCPNotificationSink} in a thread-safe TMCPSubscriptionRegistry; immediately push notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged (with _meta.subscriptionId) through the sink; return a sentinel TValue telling the transport 'stream stays open'. HTTP: in TMCPIdHTTPServer add HandlePostRequestStream: set ContentType text/event-stream + X-Accel-Buffering: no, call ResponseInfo.WriteHeader, then loop: write queued messages as 'event: message\ndata: <json>\n\n' via AContext.Connection.IOHandler.Write, write ':\r\n' every 15-30 s as keep-alive, exit on EIdSocketError/EIdConnClosedGracefully (client cancellation; mark subscription cancelled, write nothing more) or on server Stop. On Stop (before FHTTPServer.Active := False): for every open subscription write notifications/cancelled {requestId: listenId} then the completion result {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":listenId,"result":{"resultType":"complete","_meta":{"io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId":listenId}}} and close. Stdio: introduce TMCPStdioWriter (TCriticalSection around Writeln/Flush) shared by the request loop and subscription sinks; keep subscriptions in the registry; handle client 'notifications/cancelled' (currently dropped at JsonRpcProcessor.pas:174-181) by removing the subscription; on EOF emit notifications/cancelled + completion result for each active subscription before exiting. Phase B (only if dynamic registration is ever added): publish notifications/tools/list_changed etc. through the registry and flip listChanged flags to true (GAP-SUB-02).

### GAP-EXT-01 [optional, effort S] No `extensions` capability map and no place to advertise it; extension resultType values must be reserved

- Requirements: EXT-01, EXT-02, MRTR-13
- Current: Capabilities are hand-built in CoreManager.Initialize (CoreManager.pas:103-124); TMCPCapabilities in Types.pas:278-287 (with an unused FLogging TJSONObject) is dead code. No server/discover exists (other area), so there is no modern capability surface at all.
- Required: Servers advertise extensions in server/discover capabilities.extensions (map id → settings object, ids with mandatory reverse-DNS prefix); a party that supports an extension the other does not MUST fall back to core behaviour or reject; resultType values beyond complete/input_required only when the extension is advertised. `extensions` is not part of the 2025-06-18/2025-11-25 schemas.
- Compat impact: Additive: `Extensions` map empty by default (field omitted). Do not emit `extensions` in the legacy initialize result (not in those schemas; harmless but pointless). Library consumers can register extension ids via a new TMCPCapabilityBuilder.RegisterExtension(id, settings) without touching existing types.
- Suggested change: In the capability builder (GAP-SUB-02): TDictionary<string, TJSONObject> FExtensions; RegisterExtension validates the id against the _meta key rules (prefix = reverse-DNS labels, '/' separator, name [A-Za-z0-9._-]); emit 'extensions' in the modern DiscoverResult only when non-empty. Read client extensions from _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities.extensions into IMCPRequestContext.ClientExtensions: TArray<string> so managers can gate extension behaviour per request (EXT-02). Never emit a resultType other than complete/input_required unless the corresponding extension id is both registered server-side and present in the request's client extensions.

### GAP-TSK-01 [optional, effort XL] Tasks extension (io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks) not implemented — rate as optional/later

- Requirements: TSK-01, TSK-02, TSK-03, TSK-04
- Current: No tasks/* methods (→ -32601), no task store, no background execution: tools run synchronously on the Indy worker thread / stdio loop (ToolsManager.pas:276-284). No legacy tasks.* capability is advertised and the legacy `task` param is ignored (ToolsManager.pas:252-274) — which already satisfies the 'MUST ignore' rule.
- Required: If offered: advertise capabilities.extensions['io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks'] = {} in server/discover; only return CreateTaskResult (resultType 'task', taskId, status, createdAt, lastUpdatedAt, ttlMs, pollIntervalMs) to requests whose _meta clientCapabilities.extensions contains the id, after the task is durably created; serve tasks/get (status-specific DetailedTask; -32602 unknown id; -32021 for non-declaring clients), tasks/update (inputResponses keyed to outstanding inputRequests; empty ack; ignore non-outstanding keys), tasks/cancel (empty ack, cooperative); optional notifications/tasks via subscriptions/listen taskIds; unguessable ids; per-request authz; never advertise legacy tasks.* under a version that includes the extension; tasks/result → -32601; on HTTP clients send Mcp-Name = taskId (header validation is the transport area).
- Compat impact: Purely additive and opt-in (extensions are disabled by default per extensions/overview.mdx). Would introduce a new IMCPCapabilityManager (TMCPTasksManager) and a new tool-side API (e.g. TMCPToolBase.RunAsTask or a TMCPTaskContext) — existing tools untouched. Stdio single-process and HTTP multi-thread both need an in-memory TMCPTaskStore with TTL purge; a multi-instance deployment needs sticky routing on Mcp-Name (spec) or a shared store.
- Suggested change: Later phase: Managers/MCPServer.TasksManager.pas (IMCPCapabilityManagerV2) + Core/MCPServer.TaskStore.pas (TDictionary<string, TMCPTask> under TMonitor; TMCPTask {Id (TGuid + 16 random bytes, base64url), Status enum working/input_required/completed/failed/cancelled, StatusMessage, CreatedAt/LastUpdatedAt ISO-8601 UTC, TtlMs, PollIntervalMs, Result/Error TJSONObject, OutstandingInputRequests TJSONObject}); TMCPToolsManager.CallTool checks a new IMCPTaskCapableTool interface + Context.ClientExtensions contains 'io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks' → create task, start TTask.Run(execute tool; on EMCPInputRequired move to input_required with its inputRequests; on completion store result), return CreateTaskResult; if the tool REQUIRES task mode and the client did not declare it → EMCPMissingClientCapability with data.requiredCapabilities.extensions. Add the extension id to the capability builder only when a TasksManager is registered.

### GAP-RS-01 [optional, effort S] Roots/sampling: do not add server-side use; if ever exposed, only through MRTR builders with capability gating and a deprecation warning

- Requirements: RS-01, RS-02, RS-03
- Current: No roots/list or sampling/createMessage usage anywhere; no legacy server-initiated requests exist (GET stream closes at IdHTTPServer.pas:294; POST SSE emits only the response at :488-509). Conformant with the SHOULD NOTs.
- Required: New implementations SHOULD NOT adopt roots/sampling (earliest removal: first revision on/after 2027-07-28); if used they MUST only travel as inputRequests (modern), MUST respect sampling.tools / sampling.context gating, and implementations SHOULD warn when deprecated capabilities are negotiated.
- Compat impact: No change recommended. If the TMCPInputRequests builder (GAP-MRTR-01) exposes AddSampling/AddRoots for completeness, mark them `deprecated 'SEP-2577'` in Delphi so consumers get a compiler hint, and keep them behind the same capability check as elicitation.
- Suggested change: Document in README: 'Roots and sampling are deprecated in MCP 2026-07-28; this library does not implement them server-side. Pass directories via tool parameters/resource URIs/settings.ini; call LLM provider APIs directly (e.g. via System.Net.HttpClient) instead of sampling.' Optionally provide the deprecated builders as described.

### Already conformant

- MRTR-01 / patterns MUST NOT initiate requests: the server never sends JSON-RPC requests to the client — the legacy GET SSE stream closes immediately (src/Server/MCPServer.IdHTTPServer.pas:293-299) and the POST SSE path emits exactly one event, the response (IdHTTPServer.pas:488-509); stdio only ever writes responses (src/Server/MCPServer.StdioTransport.pas:621-628). MRTR-14 (InputRequiredResult must be the last SSE message) is satisfied by construction for the same reason.
- LG-01/LG-02/LG-03/LG-04 (modern) and LG-05: no `logging` capability is advertised (src/Managers/MCPServer.CoreManager.pas:103-124), no notifications/message is ever emitted, and diagnostics go to stderr in stdio mode (src/MCPServer.dpr:543-544 sets TLogger.UseStdErr; src/Core/MCPServer.Logger.pas) — exactly the SEP-2577 migration path and the stdio rule that stdout carries only MCP messages.
- RS-01/RS-02 (SHOULD NOT adopt roots/sampling): no roots/list or sampling/createMessage code exists anywhere under src/.
- EL-09: no code emits -32042, elicitationId or notifications/elicitation/complete, so the 2026-07-28 'MUST NOT emit -32042' holds.
- SUB-08/SUB-10 vacuously satisfied and truthfully advertised: resources.subscribe=false and resources.listChanged=false (CoreManager.pas:116-124), no tools.listChanged; tool and resource sets are fixed at manager construction (src/Managers/MCPServer.ToolsManager.pas:240-250; src/Managers/MCPServer.ResourcesManager.pas:497-505), so no list_changed/updated notification is ever owed in either era.
- SUB-09 / MethodNotFoundError semantics: unknown or unadvertised methods (subscriptions/listen, resources/subscribe, logging/setLevel, tasks/*, tasks/result) yield JSON-RPC -32601 via GetManagerForMethod → nil (src/Core/MCPServer.ManagerRegistry.pas:171-184; src/Protocol/MCPServer.JsonRpcProcessor.pas:121-123, 214-215) — the correct code; only the modern HTTP 404 mapping is missing (transport area).
- Notifications from the client never receive a JSON-RPC response (JsonRpcProcessor.pas:174-181) and HTTP answers 202 Accepted for notification-only bodies (IdHTTPServer.pas:343-352), matching basic/index.mdx 'The receiver MUST NOT send a response' and streamable-http.mdx item 5.
- TSK-04 'servers MUST ignore the legacy task parameter on CallToolRequest' and 'MUST NOT advertise legacy tasks.* capabilities': ExtractToolNameAndArguments reads only name/arguments (ToolsManager.pas:252-274) and no tasks capability is advertised (CoreManager.pas:103-124).
- MRTR-11 (ignore unrecognized inputResponses data): unknown params such as inputResponses/requestState are ignored rather than rejected (ToolsManager.pas:252-274; ResourcesManager.pas:548-552).
- SUB-07 partial: X-Accel-Buffering: no and Cache-Control: no-cache are already set on every SSE response (IdHTTPServer.pas:285-287, 481-483).
- Tool-level failures are reported inside the result with isError:true rather than as protocol errors (ToolsManager.pas:276-284, 307-322), matching the CallToolResult guidance in schema.ts and MRTR's 'protocol errors → JSON-RPC error' split.
- MRTR-15 (legacy clients never see InputRequiredResult) currently holds trivially because no InputRequiredResult can be produced; the gap only opens once GAP-MRTR-01 ships, hence GAP-MRTR-04.

### Notes

- Client adoption of 2026-07-28 (as of 2026-09-03, from the cloned repo): docs/extensions/client-matrix.mdx only tracks EXTENSION support (MCP Apps for Claude web/Desktop, VS Code Copilot, M365 Copilot, Goose, Postman, MCPJam, ChatGPT, Cursor, Archestra, PostHog Code; Enterprise Auth for Archestra) and says nothing about protocol revisions. blog/content/posts/2026-07-28-spec-ga/index.md: 'All four Tier 1 SDKs speak 2026-07-28 as of today' (TypeScript, Python, Go, C#), Rust SDK in beta; ecosystem quotes are server/infra side (Cloudflare Agents SDK 'day zero', Amazon Bedrock AgentCore, FastMCP 4.0, Supabase, Honeycomb, Stacklok, Manufact/mcp-use). blog 2026-06-29-sdk-betas: 'clients that speak 2026-07-28 fall back to the initialize handshake when they reach a server on 2025-11-25 or earlier'; TS/Go servers must opt in to serve 2026-07-28, Python v2 serves both eras from one endpoint, C# preview defaults to stateless. blog 2026-07-27-ruby-sdk-1-0: Ruby implements 2025-06-18 and 2025-11-25 only. No blog post or spec doc states that Claude Code, Codex, Claude Desktop or VS Code speak 2026-07-28. The MCP Inspector (docs/docs/2026-07-28/tools/inspector/protocol-eras.mdx) supports both eras but its DEFAULT protocolEra is `legacy` (plain initialize, no probing); `auto` probes server/discover and falls back; `modern` pins 2026-07-28. Conclusion: the legacy path (initialize; 2025-03-26/2025-06-18/2025-11-25) remains the primary path for Claude Code, Codex, Claude Desktop, VS Code and default Inspector; the modern path matters today for SDK-built clients, Inspector in auto/modern, and hosted platforms.
- Inspector modern-era behaviour that the server will actually meet: (1) stamps _meta['io.modelcontextprotocol/logLevel'] = 'debug' on every request by default — the server must tolerate it and 'silence is correct behavior' (GAP-LG-01); (2) 'Subscribe' sends subscriptions/listen with resourceSubscriptions + resourcesListChanged and waits for notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged, reconnecting by re-sending listen if the stream drops (GAP-SUB-01); (3) drives MRTR manually with an MRTR_MAX_ROUNDS limit; (4) notes that the legacy server.elicitInput pattern ERRORS on a 2026-07-28 connection — MRTR is the only modern elicitation path; (5) Tasks tab is gated on the negotiated io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks extension in modern era.
- Spec tension to decide on: subscriptions.mdx 'Graceful Closure' says the server SHOULD answer the listen request with a completion result on teardown, while cancellation.mdx (and schema CancelledNotification, 'On stdio') says the server MUST send notifications/cancelled referencing the listen id when tearing down the stream. Recommended ordering: stdio → notifications/cancelled then the completion result; HTTP → completion result then close the SSE stream (a notifications/cancelled on HTTP is harmless but the transport page says no client-side cancelled is expected there).
- SEP-2322 still lists GetTaskPayloadRequest (tasks/result) as an MRTR-capable request; SEP-2663 removed tasks/result. Follow mrtr.mdx's three-method table (tools/call, resources/read, prompts/get) — the normative page.
- The RC blog post (2026-05-21) shows an InputRequiredResult example with {"type":"elicitation","schema":...}; that shape is non-normative and wrong — use schema.ts ({method:'elicitation/create', params:{mode,message,requestedSchema}}) and the schema/2026-07-28/examples/InputRequiredResult/*.json files.
- MRTR retry semantics for implementers: the retry is a brand-new request (new id) carrying the ORIGINAL name/arguments plus inputResponses (+ echoed requestState). The server must reconstruct everything from that one request; SEP-2322's ADO example shows accumulating earlier answers inside requestState across rounds. Load shedding (requestState without inputRequests) is also legal.
- Delphi implementation hints: HMAC-SHA256 via System.Hash.THashSHA2.GetHMACAsBytes(Data, Key, THashSHA2.TSHA2Version.SHA256); base64url via System.NetEncoding (TBase64URLEncoding / TNetEncoding.Base64URL); random keys via System.Hash/THashSHA2 over TGuid.NewGuid + Random or Winapi BCryptGenRandom; ISO-8601 via System.DateUtils.DateToISO8601(Now, False) (Tasks). Tools are singletons shared by Indy worker threads (ToolsManager.pas:245-248) — never store per-request context in tool fields; pass IMCPRequestContext as a parameter.
- Indy long-lived SSE (needed only for subscriptions/listen): TIdHTTPServer transmits ResponseInfo after OnCommand returns, so a listen handler must call ResponseInfo.WriteHeader and then write directly to AContext.Connection.IOHandler in a loop until disconnect/shutdown; each stream pins one worker thread (set TIdHTTPServer.MaxConnections accordingly); TMCPIdHTTPServer.Stop must close streams gracefully before FHTTPServer.Active := False.
- Because capability flags are truthful (no listChanged/subscribe) the cheapest fully-compliant option for subscriptions/listen is to keep -32601 (with HTTP 404 in the modern era, transport area). The friendlier option (acknowledge with the agreed subset, possibly {}, keep the stream open with ':' keep-alives, close gracefully) costs the streaming plumbing in GAP-SUB-01 but makes Inspector/host 'Subscribe' flows succeed.
- Legacy in-stream elicitation (server sends elicitation/create on the SSE stream and waits for the client's response POST) is NOT recommended: it requires per-session request/response correlation across HTTP requests (Mcp-Session-Id), blocks an Indy thread while waiting, and is exactly what SEP-2322 replaced. Fallback for legacy clients: isError text asking for the missing argument (GAP-MRTR-04).
