- Deprecated features timeline: roots, sampling, logging are Deprecated in 2026-07-28 with earliest removal 'first revision released on or after 2027-07-28' (deprecated.mdx); wire behaviour unchanged meanwhile. Not adding them keeps the library aligned with 'New implementations SHOULD NOT adopt'.
- Type hygiene: TMCPCapabilities/TMCPToolsCapability/TMCPInitializeResponse/TMCPPingResponse/TMCPTool/TMCPToolsResponse in src/Protocol/MCPServer.Types.pas:278-337 are unused by the server; leave them for source compatibility but do not build new capability code on them (TMCPCapabilities.Logging would otherwise tempt someone to advertise the deprecated logging capability).
- Related gaps owned by other areas but prerequisites here: server/discover + supportedVersions (extensions are advertised there); resultType on every modern result; HTTP status mapping (-32021/-32022/-32020 → 400, -32601 → 404); modern per-request _meta parsing; Mcp-Method/Mcp-Name validation (tasks/* must carry Mcp-Name = taskId); removal of SSE event ids/Last-Event-ID; unknown tool/resource → -32602 in modern era (today they are isError text results / contents text).
- Open question for the orchestrator: whether the project wants a PromptsManager at all — prompts/get is one of the three MRTR-capable methods, and the tools/prompts/resources capability builder should be shared.
- Ping is removed in 2026-07-28 (changelog 5: 'Remove ping, logging/setLevel, and notifications/roots/list_changed'); the CoreManager's ping handler (CoreManager.pas:52,142-155) must remain for legacy clients but is not part of the modern surface.

### Requirements

- **MRTR-01** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST send server-to-client requests (such as roots/list, sampling/createMessage, or elicitation/create) using the MRTR pattern. The previous pattern of server-initiated requests is no longer supported." Reinforced by patterns/index.mdx: "Servers MUST NOT initiate JSON-RPC requests"; stdio.mdx: "The server MUST NOT write JSON-RPC requests to stdout"; streamable-http.mdx: "The server MUST NOT send independent JSON-RPC requests on this stream."  
  _Source: docs/specification/2026-07-28/basic/patterns/mrtr.mdx (top Note); basic/patterns/index.mdx (Overview); basic/transports/stdio.mdx (Receiving Messages); basic/transports/streamable-http.mdx (Receiving Messages)_
- **MRTR-02** (MAY, modern): "Servers MAY send InputRequiredResult responses on the following client requests: prompts/get, resources/read, tools/call." tools.mdx: "Servers MAY respond to tools/call with an InputRequiredResult"; same sentence in resources.mdx for resources/read and prompts.mdx for prompts/get.  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Supported Requests); server/tools.mdx (Input Required Tool Results); server/resources.mdx (Reading Resources); server/prompts.mdx (Getting a Prompt)_
- **MRTR-03** (MUST NOT, modern): "Servers MUST NOT send InputRequiredResult responses on any other client requests." (i.e. not on tools/list, resources/list, resources/templates/list, prompts/list, server/discover, completion/complete, subscriptions/listen).  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Supported Requests)_
- **MRTR-04** (MUST, modern): "inputRequests keys are server assigned identifiers and MUST be unique within the scope of the request." "inputRequests values are request objects that MUST be one of ElicitRequest, CreateMessageRequest, or ListRootsRequest" (each is {method, params}).  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements (Basic Workflow) item 2); schema/2026-07-28/schema.ts InputRequest/InputRequests_
- **MRTR-05** (MUST, modern): "If a client request contains a requestState field, servers MUST treat requestState as an attacker-controlled input. If requestState influences authorization, resource access, or business logic, servers MUST protect its integrity (e.g. HMAC or AEAD) and MUST reject state that fails verification. Integrity protection MAY be omitted only when tampering can cause nothing worse than request failure."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements item 4; Security Considerations)_
- **MRTR-06** (SHOULD, modern): "To prevent replay, servers SHOULD include the following inside the integrity-protected requestState payload and verify each on receipt: the authenticated principal ...; a short expiry (TTL) ...; an identifier for the originating request, e.g. the method name and a digest of its salient parameters". Warning: "Servers for which a given requestState must be consumed at most once ... MUST enforce that invariant server-side."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements item 5)_
- **MRTR-07** (MUST, modern): "Servers MUST include at least one of inputRequests or requestState in every InputRequiredResult response." Schema: InputRequiredResult { resultType: "input_required"; inputRequests?; requestState?: string }.  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements item 6); schema.ts InputRequiredResult_
- **MRTR-08** (MUST NOT, modern): "Servers MUST NOT send an inputRequests that the client has not declared support for in its capabilities. For example, if a client does not declare support for elicitation, the server MUST NOT include any elicitation/create requests." basic/index.mdx: "If processing a request requires a capability the client did not include in io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities, the server MUST return a MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError (-32021) whose data.requiredCapabilities lists the missing capabilities. On HTTP, the response status MUST be 400 Bad Request." schema: "Servers MUST NOT infer capabilities from prior requests."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements item 7); basic/index.mdx (_meta, Per-request protocol fields); schema.ts RequestMetaObject.clientCapabilities, MISSING_REQUIRED_CLIENT_CAPABILITY = -32021, MissingRequiredClientCapabilityError_
- **MRTR-09** (MUST NOT, modern): "Servers MUST NOT assume that clients will fulfill the inputRequests or retry the original request. Servers MAY choose to return an InputRequiredResult on multiple attempts at the same request". Retry is an independent request: "The JSON-RPC id MUST be different between the initial request and the retry" (client side) and "the server processing the retry does not need any information beyond what is directly present in the retry request".  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Server Requirements item 8; Basic Workflow)_
- **MRTR-10** (SHOULD, modern): "Servers SHOULD validate that the data provided by the client is a valid InputResponses object and that the information inside can be correctly parsed. Protocol errors (malformed JSON, invalid schema, internal server errors) SHOULD return a JSON-RPC error response with an appropriate error code and message."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Error Handling)_
- **MRTR-11** (SHOULD, modern): "If additional, unexpected parameters are provided in the InputResponses object, the server SHOULD ignore any information it does not recognize or need."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Error Handling)_
- **MRTR-12** (SHOULD, modern): "If the client fails to send all the information requested in a previous InputRequests, and the missing information is necessary for the server to process the request, the server SHOULD respond with a new InputRequiredResult requesting the missing information again, rather than returning an error."  
  _Source: mrtr.mdx (Error Handling)_
- **MRTR-13** (MUST, modern): "The result MUST include a resultType field to indicate the type of the result." "Servers implementing this protocol version MUST include this field." Values: "complete" for ordinary results, "input_required" for InputRequiredResult; "Extensions MAY add additional ResultType values. The set of supported ResultType values MUST be created from the set defined in the core protocol and include any additional values of supported extensions that are advertised via capabilities."  
  _Source: basic/index.mdx (Responses; ResultType); schema.ts Result.resultType; changelog.mdx (Major changes 8)_
- **MRTR-14** (MUST NOT, modern): SEP-2322: InputRequiredResult "MAY be sent either as a standalone response or as the final message on an SSE stream, although implementations are encouraged to prefer the former. If using an SSE stream, servers MUST NOT send any message on the stream after the incomplete response message." streamable-http.mdx: "The final JSON-RPC response SHOULD terminate the stream."  
  _Source: seps/2322-MRTR.md (Protocol Requirements for Ephemeral Workflow, Server Behavior); streamable-http.mdx (Receiving Messages)_
- **MRTR-15** (MUST, legacy): Legacy sessions know nothing of resultType/InputRequiredResult: in 2025-03-26..2025-11-25 server-to-client requests are JSON-RPC requests on an SSE stream ("The server MAY send JSON-RPC requests and notifications before sending the JSON-RPC response"), and "Both parties MUST: Respect the negotiated protocol version; Only use capabilities that were successfully negotiated." versioning.mdx: "An initialize request selects legacy semantics, scoped to the stdio process (stdio) or the session (HTTP), as specified by the negotiated legacy protocol version." => a dual-era server MUST NOT emit InputRequiredResult (resultType input_required) to a legacy-era client.  
  _Source: docs/specification/2025-06-18/basic/lifecycle.mdx (Operation); 2025-06-18/basic/transports.mdx (Sending Messages to the Server item 104-106); 2026-07-28/basic/versioning.mdx (Backward Compatibility, dual-era server selects behavior)_
- **EL-01** (MUST NOT, both): "Servers MUST NOT use form mode elicitation to request sensitive information such as passwords, API keys, access tokens, or payment credentials"; "Servers MUST use URL mode for interactions involving such sensitive information". Also Form Mode Security 1: "Servers MUST NOT request sensitive information (passwords, API keys, etc.) via form mode".  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (User Interaction Model Warning; Form Mode Security); identical text in 2025-11-25/client/elicitation.mdx; 2025-06-18: "Servers MUST NOT use elicitation to request sensitive information"_
- **EL-02** (MUST NOT, both): "Servers MUST NOT send elicitation requests with modes that are not supported by the client." "an empty capabilities object is equivalent to declaring support for form mode only" (elicitation: {} == {form: {}}). In 2026-07-28 the capability is declared per request in _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities; in 2025-11-25 during initialize; in 2025-06-18 elicitation is `object` (form only, no mode field).  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Capabilities); 2025-11-25/client/elicitation.mdx (Capabilities); schema/2025-06-18/schema.ts:250 elicitation?: object_
- **EL-03** (MUST, modern): "All elicitation requests MUST include the following parameters: mode (optional for form, defaults to form), message." "Form mode elicitation requests MUST either specify mode: \"form\" or omit the mode field, and include ... requestedSchema." "URL mode elicitation requests MUST specify mode: \"url\", a message, and include ... url"; "The url parameter MUST contain a valid URL." requestedSchema is "limited to flat objects with primitive properties only" (string/number/integer/boolean/enum incl. multi-select arrays).  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Elicitation Requests; Form Mode Elicitation Requests; Requested Schema; URL Mode Elicitation Requests); schema.ts ElicitRequest, PrimitiveSchemaDefinition_
- **EL-04** (MAY, modern): "Servers MAY request information from a user during the processing of a client request, by sending an InputRequiredResult containing an elicitation/create request." Client result inside inputResponses is ElicitResult {action: accept|decline|cancel; content?}. For URL mode, action accept "does not mean that the interaction is complete"; on retry "the server determines from the echoed requestState (or its own stored state) whether the out-of-band interaction has completed, and either returns the final result or responds with another InputRequiredResult."  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Elicitation Requests; URL Mode Elicitation Requests; Response Actions)_
- **EL-05** (MUST, both): "Servers SHOULD NOT assume that elicitation requests will always succeed, and MUST handle cases where the user declines or cancels the elicitation, or where the client fails to process the request."  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Error Handling)_
- **EL-06** (MUST, both): "Servers MUST bind elicitation requests to the client and user identity"; "if state is stored, servers implementing elicitation MUST securely associate this state with individual users ... State storage MUST be protected against unauthorized access; For remote MCP servers, user identification MUST be derived from credentials acquired via MCP authorization when possible (e.g. sub claim)"; "Servers MUST NOT rely on client-provided user identification without server verification".  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Security Considerations 1; Statefulness; Identifying the User)_
- **EL-07** (MUST NOT, both): Safe URL Handling — servers requesting elicitation: "MUST NOT include sensitive information about the end-user ... in the URL"; "MUST NOT provide a URL which is pre-authenticated to access a protected resource"; "SHOULD NOT include URLs intended to be clickable in any field of a form mode elicitation request"; "SHOULD use HTTPS URLs for non-development environments". Phishing: "The MCP Server MUST verify the identity of the user who opens the URL before accepting information" and "MUST ensure that the user who started the elicitation request ... is the same user who completes the authorization flow"; "MUST ensure that the mechanism to determine the user's identity is resilient to attacks where an attacker can modify the elicitation URL." OAuth: "MCP servers MUST NOT rely on URL mode elicitation to authorize users for themselves"; "The MCP server MUST NOT transmit credentials obtained through URL mode elicitation to the MCP client."  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Safe URL Handling; Phishing; URL Mode Elicitation for OAuth Flows)_
- **EL-08** (SHOULD, both): "Servers SHOULD validate received data matches the requested schema" (Form Mode Security 3).  
  _Source: client/elicitation.mdx (Form Mode Security)_
- **EL-09** (MUST NOT, both): 2026-07-28 removes the 2025-11-25 URLElicitationRequiredError and elicitationId: "Implementations of this protocol version MUST NOT emit these codes: ... -32042 — URL elicitation required (2025-11-25 only)." changelog 11: removes notifications/elicitation/complete and the elicitationId field; "Servers needing to correlate an elicitation across retries encode their own identifier in requestState." (Legacy 2025-11-25: server MAY return -32042 only when URL mode elicitation is required and MAY send notifications/elicitation/complete.)  
  _Source: basic/index.mdx (Error Codes); changelog.mdx (Minor changes 11); 2025-11-25/client/elicitation.mdx (URL Elicitation Required Error; Completion Notifications)_

Earliest removal: first revision released on or after 2027-07-28. If used: "servers send an InputRequiredResult containing a roots/list request"; servers SHOULD "Check for roots capability before usage". notifications/roots/list_changed is removed in 2026-07-28 (changelog 5).

"During the deprecation period, wire-level behavior is unchanged."

Legacy: for logging/setLevel "Servers SHOULD return standard JSON-RPC errors for common failure cases: Invalid log level: -32602" (only meaningful when logging capability is advertised; otherwise -32601 per schema MethodNotFoundError: "one gated behind a server capability the server did not advertise").

"The notifications field in the acknowledgment reflects the subset the server agreed to honor. Notification types the server does not support are omitted."

Result: {"resultType":"complete","_meta":{"io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId": <listen id>}} — schema SubscriptionsListenResult makes _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId REQUIRED.

Servers MUST NOT send notifications/cancelled for any other purpose." schema: "On stdio, the server also sends this notification, solely to terminate a subscriptions/listen stream ... Servers MUST NOT use this notification to cancel any other request."

- **SUB-06** (SHOULD, modern): Client cancellation of a listen stream: HTTP — "Closing the SSE response stream MUST be treated by the server as cancellation of that request ... The server SHOULD stop work on the cancelled request as soon as practical and MUST NOT send any further messages for it." stdio — client sends notifications/cancelled referencing the listen request id; "Servers receiving cancellation notifications SHOULD: Stop processing the cancelled request; Free associated resources; Not send a response for the cancelled request"; "Servers MAY ignore cancellation notifications if: The referenced request is unknown; Processing has already completed". "On stdio, if the connection is terminated and then re-established, the client MUST re-send subscriptions/listen ... the server holds no subscription state across reconnections."

"Resumable SSE streams via Last-Event-ID are not supported." Request-scoped notifications (progress, message) "are not delivered on the listen stream".

- **SUB-08** (SHOULD, modern): "When the list of available tools changes, servers that declared the listChanged capability SHOULD send a notification to clients that have opened a subscriptions/listen stream with toolsListChanged: true" (notifications/tools/list_changed); identical for prompts (promptsListChanged) and resources (resourcesListChanged; resources/updated for resourceSubscriptions). resources capability: "subscribe: whether the server supports resource-specific update notifications for resources requested through subscriptions/listen using the resourceSubscriptions filter"; "Servers may advertise either feature independently, together or neither."

On HTTP (modern): "If the server does not implement the requested RPC method, it MUST respond with 404 Not Found and a JSON-RPC error with code -32601."

Modern-only servers: "HTTP GET or DELETE to the MCP endpoint: respond with 405 Method Not Allowed" (SHOULD).

- **EXT-01** (MUST, modern): "Extensions are advertised in the extensions field of capabilities, which is a map of extension identifiers to per-extension settings objects. Extension identifiers MUST follow the _meta key naming rules, with a mandatory prefix." "Each extension specifies the schema of its settings object; an empty object indicates support with no additional settings." Servers advertise in server/discover result capabilities.extensions; clients in _meta.io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities.extensions on each request. "Extensions are always disabled by default and require explicit opt-in from the developer." The extensions field does not exist in the 2025-06-18 / 2025-11-25 schemas.

- **EXT-02** (MUST, modern): "If one party supports an extension but the other does not, the supporting party MUST either revert to core protocol behavior or reject the request with an appropriate error. Extensions SHOULD document their expected fallback behavior." Extension resultType values may only be used when the extension is advertised via capabilities (see MRTR-13).  
  _Source: basic/versioning.mdx (Extension Negotiation); basic/index.mdx (ResultType)_
- **TSK-01** (MAY, modern): Tasks extension io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks: "A server that has negotiated this extension MAY return CreateTaskResult in lieu of a standard result (e.g. CallToolResult) in response to any supported request [tools/call] at its own discretion and on a per-request basis." "A server that returns the standard CallToolResult shape — i.e., never elects to create a task — remains fully spec-compliant under this extension."  
  _Source: seps/2663-tasks-extension.md (Capability Negotiation; Supported Methods; Backward Compatibility); docs/extensions/tasks/overview.mdx_
- **TSK-02** (MUST NOT, modern): "A server MUST NOT return CreateTaskResult to a client that did not include the extension capability on its request, regardless of prior declarations." "If a server is unable to service a request to a client that does not declare this extension capability without returning CreateTaskResult, the server MUST return an error with the code -32021 ... data.requiredCapabilities.extensions['io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks']". "Servers MUST set resultType to \"task\" when returning a CreateTaskResult ... MUST NOT set resultType to \"task\" on result types other than CreateTaskResult."  
  _Source: seps/2663-tasks-extension.md (Capability Negotiation; Polymorphic Results)_
- **TSK-03** (MUST, modern): If implemented: "A server MUST NOT return CreateTaskResult until the task is durably created"; tasks/get "MUST" return the status-specific Task shape (working | input_required + inputRequests | completed + result | failed + error | cancelled), resultType "complete"; tasks/update: "the server MUST acknowledge the request with an empty result", "SHOULD ignore any inputResponses ... not currently outstanding"; tasks/cancel: "The server MUST acknowledge the request with an empty result", cooperative; errors: "Invalid or nonexistent taskId: -32602 ... Servers MUST return this error for tasks/get ... SHOULD ... for tasks/update and tasks/cancel"; "-32021 ... MUST return this error for non-declaring clients issuing tasks/get, tasks/update, and tasks/cancel" and for non-declaring clients requesting taskIds on subscriptions/listen; "Each request key in inputRequests MUST be unique over the lifetime of a single task"; "The failed status MUST NOT be used to represent non-JSON-RPC errors" (isError results are completed); HTTP: "the client MUST set the Mcp-Name header ... to the value of params.taskId"; security: task IDs "MUST generate them with sufficient entropy"; "Servers MUST perform authentication and authorization checks on each task-related request"; "notifications/progress and notifications/message notifications MUST NOT be sent on the subscriptions/listen stream for a task"; MRTR before task: "SHOULD resolve all MRTR exchanges synchronously before responding with a CreateTaskResult".
